### `.5.e` DESIGN SPIKE (session #104, 2026-07-12, `PGEN-RGX-0078-0034`, docs-only) — GLL REFUTED as a speed rung; the destination gap is measured ARCHITECTURAL (≈99× vs the `<5×` bound)

**Method.** The leaf's own mandate ([[feedback_research_grounded_sota_no_trial_and_revert]] + the `.5.e` status line): survey the
GLL/Thompson-NFA/SPPF literature FIRST, produce a worked mapping to the PGEN engine, and only then build. Applying the `.5.d.2`/`.5.d.3`
lesson (measure the marginal premise BEFORE building the high-risk change), the spike also measured, for the first time, the actual
distance to the road's destination. Nothing was built; nothing needs reverting.

#### A. Tool facts (this session, all re-runnable)

1. **Probe sanity (drift control).** `regex_perf_probe --samples 2000 --warmup 200` at HEAD (`250c94c3`, fat-LTO sha `d954f897…`):
   geomean-of-mins ≈ **56.6µs** — reproduces the pinned `.5.g` ≈56.8µs. Baseline valid.
2. **Distance to destination (NEW — the first PGEN-vs-PCRE2 measurement on the bench corpus).** `pcre2test -t 10000` (PCRE2 10.47,
   same machine, full `pcre2_compile` per pattern) on the same 8 patterns:
   | pattern | PGEN min | PCRE2 compile | ratio |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | literal_simple | 11.79µs | 0.337µs | 35× |
   | digit_sequence | 54.96µs | 0.487µs | 113× |
   | character_class | 145.46µs | 0.851µs | 171× |
   | alternation | 27.08µs | 0.513µs | 53× |
   | capture_groups | 133.71µs | 0.698µs | 192× |
   | url_simple | 32.92µs | 0.490µs | 67× |
   | email_basic | 62.08µs | 0.526µs | 118× |
   | anchor_complex | 150.83µs | 0.859µs | 176× |
   **geomean(PGEN/PCRE2) ≈ 99×** (PCRE2 geomean ≈0.57µs). Honest bounds: 8-pattern preview, NOT the official `.6` corpus metric
   (needs `pgen_iteration_flow` vendored); PGEN=min vs PCRE2=mean-of-10k — a second-order caveat that cannot move a 99× verdict.
   At activation (496µs) the distance was ≈870×; the five landed levers closed 8.76×; **reaching `<5×` needs a further ≈20×.**
3. **The arithmetic that kills ALL bucket levers.** RE-PROFILE #4's remaining named buckets — rule-call ~12% (distributed, no
   hotspot) + SEM residual ~8% + SERDE ~6% + Vec/clones ~4.5% + SipHash residual ~1.5% ≈ **32%** ⇒ eliminating EVERY named bucket
   entirely yields ≈1.5×, vs the needed ≈20×. No composition of profile-bucket levers reaches the destination.
4. **WHERE the ~20× actually lives (the architectural WHY+WHERE).** `PGEN_REPORT_MEMO_STATS=1 parseability_probe --parse regex` on
   the 4-char pattern `test`: **45 rule entries** (≈11/char — the `piece→atom→literal→literal_char→letter` cascade + the failing
   `quantifier`/`zero_width`/`unicode_char` probes per char), at 11.79µs ⇒ **≈262ns per rule entry** (memo probe + first-set check +
   runtime wrapper + node/arena + logger per entry). PCRE2 compiles the ENTIRE pattern (0.337µs) for roughly the cost of ONE PGEN
   rule entry. The dominant cost is the **rule-cascade-per-char execution model** itself — the ~66% of self-time that RE-PROFILE #4
   left distributed/unnamed is this machinery, addressable only by executing FEWER rule entries, not by shaving any bucket.

#### B. Literature survey (the research-grounded GLL adjudication)

- **GLL** [Scott & Johnson, *GLL Parsing*, ENTCS 253(7) 2010]: generalizes RD to ALL CFGs via descriptors + a graph-structured
  stack (GSS); O(n³) worst case. Its value = handling nondeterminism/ambiguity that would make plain RD exponential or incorrect.
- **Optimized GLL** [Afroozeh & Izmaylova, *Faster, Practical GLL Parsing*, CC 2015 (Iguana)]: a more efficient GSS + optimizations,
  evaluated on highly-ambiguous + real programming-language grammars — the strongest practical GLL.
- **The decisive comparison** [Parr, Harwell, Fisher, *Adaptive LL(\*) Parsing: The Power of Dynamic Analysis*, OOPSLA 2014]:
  ALL(*) — top-down parsing with adaptive lookahead-DFA-cached prediction — "**outperforms general strategies such as GLL and GLR
  by orders of magnitude**" on grammars used in practice (e.g. 3.2MB Java in 360ms vs GLL/GLR-family tools needing GBs + far longer;
  their own data shows the prediction CACHE is what makes it viable — uncached simulation is 100–1000× slower).
- **Worked mapping to PGEN.** Post-`.5.c.2`, PGEN's engine already occupies the ALL(*)-side of this divide: memoized (packrat)
  recursive descent + STATIC k=1 first-set pruning + delta-based winner-only store effects. The regex grammar is deterministic
  (tournament winner selection, no ambiguity — a true `(rule,start,end)` SPPF has nothing to share); the surviving-branch sets after
  first-set pruning are singleton-or-small. GLL's machinery would REPLACE cheap native call/return with heap-allocated descriptors +
  GSS node/edge bookkeeping + SPPF construction on EVERY rule entry — i.e., it ADDS a per-entry constant to the exact ~262ns/entry
  floor that IS the problem, in exchange for removing a backtrack residue measured at **~2.2%** (`rollback_to_named` 1.30% +
  `extract_delta_since` 0.85%). Even the adjacent within-family upgrade (ALL(*)-style adaptive k>1 prediction caching) targets only
  the residual failing probes (~38% of entries on `test` fail, but each fails at char 1 — already first-char-cheap).
- **VERDICT: REFUTED.** Predicted net-NEGATIVE (bookkeeping added ≫ residue removed), against a ≤3% honest ceiling; it cannot land
  under the ⛔ HARD CONSTRAINT (land iff faster + byte-identical). Same adjudication class as `.5.d.3` (premise falsified before
  building — the doctrine working as designed, zero trial-and-revert cost).

#### C. Road-level steering (SURFACED to the director — no unilateral re-sequencing; [[feedback_stick_to_agreed_plan_no_silent_drift]])

The agreed road said "`.5.g` then `.5.e`"; `.5.e`'s spike is complete and its premise is refuted, which puts the ROAD itself at a
decision point: **no remaining `.5` bucket lever can reach the `<5×` destination — the residual ≈20× is architectural.** The three
honest options (any combination):

1. **`.7` PARSE-CACHE + BATCH (recommended next).** Already queued, director-originated. The real RGX workload recompiles repeated
   patterns; a persistent pattern→AST cache makes the amortized per-pattern cost ≈0 (a cache hit is a hash + clone — plausibly
   ~1µs-scale, i.e. UNDER the 5× bound on the workload metric). Prerequisite either way: **vendor `pgen_iteration_flow`** so `.6`'s
   OFFICIAL corpus metric exists (this spike's 99× is the 8-pattern preview). Low risk, measurable, workload-honest.
2. **CASCADE FUSION / grammar-derived scanner compilation (the honest research-grade REPLACEMENT for GLL).** Attack the
   entries-per-char product directly: codegen-fuse LINEAR single-alternative wrapper chains (e.g. `literal→literal_char→letter`)
   and/or compile token-shaped regular sublanguages into direct-coded scanners that emit the identical arena nodes + typed JSON
   (byte-identical oracle unchanged). Ceiling if entries/char drop ~11→~2: ~3–5× on the bench — REAL but still short of 20× alone;
   sequence AFTER `.7` if the raw-latency race is still on once the workload metric exists. STEP-0 gate before any build: a
   fusibility census (count rule entries on the bench corpus that sit on single-alt chains / token-shaped rules).
3. **RE-BASELINE the `.6` closure bound (director decision).** `<5×` RAW vs a hand-written single-pass C compiler that emits no
   AST may be the wrong contract for a general grammar-driven parser emitting a typed JSON AST through annotations; candidate
   honest form: cache-amortized `<5×` on the real RGX iteration workload + a raw per-parse budget (e.g. geomean ≤ N µs).

**Recommendation: 1 → then adjudicate 2/3 with the official metric in hand.** Destination intent (make PGEN-regex fast enough that
speed is a non-issue for RGX) unchanged; this is a route correction surfaced BEFORE any action, per the standing discipline.

#### D. The DERIVED-SCANNER design sketch (director Q&A, session #105, 2026-07-13 — pre-decision, recorded so it survives; NO leaf opened)

Answering the director's "PGEN has no lexer/tokenizer — can we keep EBNF as sole source of truth, stay 100% parser-neutral, AND
benefit from a lexer+tokenizer?" — **YES, by DERIVING the scanner from the grammar, never declaring one**:

- **WHAT'S WRONG with the current model:** the full general-parser apparatus (memo probe + observability + semantic-runtime wrapper +
  tournament + node build + furthest tracking ≈ 262ns) is paid PER RULE ENTRY, and entries go down to single characters (~11/char on
  the bench) — finite-state recognition executed through a general rule engine at ~100× its intrinsic cost.
- **THE FAST SHAPE (layered execution, one grammar):** bottom layer = the grammar's REGULAR subtrees compiled to direct-coded DFAs
  (re2c/Ragel form, ~ns/char, no memo — linearity holds by construction); top layer = structural/annotated/store-gated rules keep the
  existing RD machinery but consume TOKENS ⇒ rule-entry cost per token, not per char.
- **THE CAPABILITY GATE (static, over the gen-AST — parser-AGNOSTIC, never name-gated):** a subtree is scanner-compilable iff
  (1) regular (no ref cycles; iteration via quantifiers only); (2) no outcome/store-affecting directives inside; (3) AST contribution
  reconstructible from matched text (increment 1 = only text-folding token rules — strictest); (4) choice semantics DFA-encodable —
  `longest_match` IS maximal munch (accept-state branch tagging; `a|ab` identical), `ordered`/`priority` = accept-priority tie-break;
  (5) grammar-declared layout policy respected at the boundary. NOT a pre-pass: each scanner is invoked exactly where the cascade
  would have been ⇒ context-dependent tokenization (the reason PGEN is scannerless) preserved BY CONSTRUCTION.
- **DUALITY untouched** (execution-only change; stimuli generation walks the same EBNF). **Pre-registered risks (pause-and-agree):**
  (R-i) rule-participation TESTIMONY must be synthesized by the fused scanner (cert coverage + contract gates consume it);
  (R-ii) furthest_position parity on rejection; (R-iii) gate breadth per grammar unknown until the census.
- **ROAD (the proven method):** STEP 0 = mechanical FUSIBILITY CENSUS over all shipped grammars (the ceiling, measured BEFORE any
  build — the `.5.d.3`/`.5.e` discipline) → DFA construction (Thompson/subset over the gen-AST; FIRST-set machinery already computes
  on this IR) → codegen emission (identical folded AST + testimony + furthest) → byte-identical increments (leaves → chains →
  keyword/alternation sets → deeper), interpreter stays UNFUSED as the fresh-walking reference oracle (the `.5.g` pointed-oracle
  pattern). Literature anchors for the spike: re2c/Ragel/flex (derived direct-coded scanners); context-aware scanning
  [Van Wyk & Schwerdfeger, Copper] (parser-context-informed lexing); ANTLR (derived lexer ATNs).
- **Honest ceiling:** ~70–80% fused entries ⇒ ~3–5× on the bench (→ ~12–19µs); raw `<5×` vs PCRE2 on FIRST-SEEN patterns remains
  uncertain ⇒ COMPOSES WITH (does not replace) `.7` parse-cache for the workload goal. SV/VHDL/JSON likely gain MORE than regex
  (multi-char tokens). This sketch = the concrete form of steering option (2); the road pick remains PENDING with the director.
- **DIRECTOR REQUIREMENT (stated 2026-07-13, session #105 — the design's acceptance frame):** *"keep the sole source of truth +
  return annotation + semantic annotation and still use a lexer and tokenizer in the background"* — satisfied by construction:
  the author-facing contract (one `.ebnf`, return + semantic annotations) is UNCHANGED; the scanner is a codegen-computed
  execution strategy (like FIRST sets / LR elimination), invisible to authors. Semantic-annotated/store-gated rules are excluded
  from fusion by the gate and run on the full runtime; token-shaped text-folding return annotations are applied by the scanner to
  the same matched text (byte-identical). The boundary is ADAPTIVE: adding a directive to a fused rule automatically un-fuses that
  subtree at the next regeneration — the grammar's own properties manage the lexer, never the author.

#### E. BRAINSTORM REGISTER (director + engineer, session #105, 2026-07-13 — pre-decision idea capture; NOTHING committed to build)

1. **Scanner second-order payoffs (same fusibility machinery):** (a) a token-DFA is BIDIRECTIONAL — sampling accepting paths =
   token-layer STIMULI GENERATION (duality-complete primitive; possible lever on witnessing-wall classes); (b) EXPECTED-TOKEN
   diagnostics from DFA states ("expected one of …" at rejects — a user-facing quality win beyond `furthest_position`); (c) the
   fusibility census as a STANDING grammar metric ("token-shapedness", tracked like cert coverage; flags needlessly irregular rules).
2. **Residual engine ideas (scanner-independent):** (a) ADAPTIVE/LAZY MEMOIZATION — after first-set pruning, measure whether memo
   probes still pay rent (`PGEN_REPORT_MEMO_STATS` on the bench; literature: Nez elastic packrat window, cut-operator memo elision);
   fused regions need no memo at all (DFA linear by construction); (b) TYPED/ZERO-COPY OUTPUT path in the embedding API (the ~6%
   SERDE bucket; typed visitor / borrowed carrier; contract-ADDITIVE; `regex_typed_*` probes hint RGX interest); (c) SIMD byte-class
   scanning INSIDE fused scanners later (Hyperscan/re2c playbook — an increment of the scanner rung, not a rung).
3. **⭐ THE WORKLOAD TRACE (pull hardest):** `.6` races a NUMBER without a TRACE. If RGX caches compiled matchers, PGEN parse cost
   hits only COLD patterns ⇒ the honest closure metric is consumer-relative ("PGEN ≤ X% of RGX's end-to-end pattern-compile
   pipeline") or amortized-under-recurrence. ONE real RGX workload trace (pattern arrival + recurrence histogram) converts `.6`
   from a number race into an SLO — and may show "speed is a non-issue" is closer than 99× suggests. `pgen_iteration_flow`
   vendoring = the instrument either way.
4. **Meta-lever — cheapen measurement:** every land-iff-faster verdict costs 2× ~8–13min fat-LTO builds; archive baseline binaries
   per landed rung / thin-LTO for iteration + fat-LTO only for landing measures. The campaign bottleneck is shifting from finding
   levers to EVALUATING them.
5. **Framings:** (a) Futamura — generated parser = interpreter specialized to the grammar; the scanner rung = the SECOND
   specialization (to structure classes); GLL was the opposite direction (more runtime generality) — the deep reason it refuted
   cleanly; (b) SV may win MORE than regex (multi-char tokens + SV dominates gate wall-clock ⇒ platform-velocity gain); log the
   generated-code-SIZE risk (SV artifact already 72MB — big keyword DFAs table-driven, small ones direct-coded).
6. **Char-level vs token-level (director question, tool-verified):** architecturally ALL PGEN parsers are scannerless; in PRACTICE
   other grammars' token-shaped leaves are `/.../` regex-literal terminals — SV has ~410, compiled to **323 `match_regex` sites** =
   ONE Rust-regex-engine call per token (a de-facto leaf-scanner) — while the regex parser has **0** (the REGEX-SELF-HOSTING
   directive converted all 36 `/.../` to pure char-level rules) ⇒ regex pays rule machinery PER CHARACTER, twice-compounded by the
   language itself being character-grained. **NEW INSIGHT: the derived scanner UNIFIES both leaf mechanisms and GENERALIZES
   SELF-HOSTING platform-wide** — PGEN-derived DFAs replace BOTH the char cascades (regex) AND the 323 external-engine
   `match_regex` calls (SV/VHDL/…), removing the Rust-regex crate dependency for EVERY parser (today only regex is held to that
   bar) while likely beating the general-purpose engine per call. The scanner rung = a speed lever AND the completion of the
   self-hosting doctrine.
- Engineer's suggested ordering (non-binding): 3 first (one trace can re-price everything) → scanner rung (payoffs 1a–1c compound)
  → 4 alongside as cheap infrastructure.
- **⛔ DIRECTOR STEER (2026-07-13, session #105):** register item 3 (the RGX workload trace /
  consumer-relative reframe) **SHALL NOT be considered** — "I can't control RGX, but I can control how fast PGEN runs; focus all
  attention on PGEN." NEW DATA POINT (director, RGX-side): **PGEN was ≈80% of RGX's overall compile slowness** — timestamp
  **RESOLVED (director, 2026-07-13): measured PRE-campaign, around the time RGX logged the issue report** (the ≈496µs era)
  ⇒ the landed 8.76× has ALREADY cut RGX's PGEN share ≈80%→≈31% and RGX's overall compile ≈3.4× (assuming the non-PGEN 20%
  unchanged); further PGEN speedups yield ≤≈1.46× more on RGX's pipeline (Amdahl on the ≈31% residual). The scanner rung's
  justification therefore rests on PGEN's OWN first-class speed deliverable (the north star) + that residual — consistent
  with the director's steer. Consequence: the road collapses to the PGEN-native raw-speed lever — the DERIVED-SCANNER rung.

#### F. AGREEMENT (2026-07-13, session #105) — the DERIVED-SCANNER rung is the agreed road; `.5.h` OPENED

Director, after the pull-based interface walk-through: *"the way you explained it kind of reassured me about the viability of the
process. So let's say we reached an agreement here."* The AGREED design (full detail = sections C/D/E + this interface contract):

- **Codegen (the AST pipeline = the compiler; it gains two stages, same gen-AST input):** (1) a FUSIBILITY pass (the section-D
  capability gate) marks scanner-compilable subtrees; (2) a SCANNER EMITTER builds a DFA per maximal fusible subtree (Thompson/
  subset over the rule bodies; accept states TAGGED by rule/branch; maximal munch == `longest_match`, tie-breaks encoded) and
  emits it as direct-coded `scan_*` functions INTO the same generated parser file.
- **Parse time (the communication contract):** PULL-BASED, **NO token stream, no pre-pass**. At exactly the site where the parser
  would have descended into a fused rule R, the emitted code calls `scan_R(pos) -> (accept_tag, len)` and BUILDS THE SAME
  ParseNode/folded value `parse_R` would have built from that span. Callers unchanged; AST byte-identical; context-dependent
  tokenization correct by construction (a different grammar position invokes a different scanner).
- **Two fusion increments:** (i) token-RULE fusion (each token-shaped rule body → one scan fn; also replaces the ~323 external
  `match_regex` Rust-regex-engine calls in SV etc. ⇒ platform-wide self-hosting); (ii) MERGED-CHOICE scanning (a choice site whose
  branches are all token-shaped → ONE multi-accept DFA scan replaces the N-branch tournament). Regex's win = increment (i) applied
  to its per-char cascade (char-grained "tokens" — the director's distinction, correct).
- **Untouched invariants:** `.ebnf` + return/semantic annotations (authors see NOTHING); gen-AST IR; semantic runtime/store
  (store-gated rules never fuse; adaptive boundary un-fuses on directive addition); typed JSON AST + schema (byte-identical, the
  ⛔ oracle floor); packrat memo for structural rules (unneeded inside scanners — DFA linear); the INTERPRETER stays rule-walking
  as the reference oracle; stimuli generation/duality unaffected.
- **OUT of scope (explicitly):** a batch pre-lexing mode (context-independent-token grammars only; maybe-later, capability-gated,
  only if pull-based measurably leaves wins on the table). GLL stays refuted. `.7` cache re-sequenced AFTER the rung;
  `.6` re-baseline dropped.
- **Pre-registered risks carried forward (pause-and-agree per the standing protocol):** (R-i) participation-testimony synthesis
  (cert coverage + contract gates), (R-ii) `furthest_position` parity on rejection, (R-iii) per-grammar gate breadth unknown until
  the census, (R-iv) generated-code size (SV 72MB — big keyword DFAs table-driven, small direct-coded).

- ID: `.5.h`  Status: `parked` (session #107 — speed claim measurement-refuted by `.5.h.1`+`.5.h.1b`; the
  `match_regex` self-hosting surface stays available for explicit prioritization; see §G.1 RESOLUTION + §H)
  (was: OPENED at director agreement, 2026-07-13, session #105)  Goal: **THE DERIVED-SCANNER RUNG** —
  compile the grammar's regular, annotation-free subtrees into direct-coded DFA `scan_*` functions emitted into the generated
  parser (pull-based scan-and-build; the section-F contract). Parser-AGNOSTIC capability gate; ⛔ HARD CONSTRAINT per increment
  (land iff faster + byte-identical, full oracle battery). Honest ceiling estimate ~3–5× on the bench (census will replace the
  estimate with a measurement). Children: `.5.h.1`, `.5.h.1b`.
  - ID: `.5.h.1`  Status: `done` (session #106, 2026-07-13 — tools BUILT + census MEASURED for all 11 shipped grammars; see the
    `.5.h.1` CENSUS section below)  Goal: **STEP-0 FUSIBILITY CENSUS** — build the capability-gate
    classifier as an analysis over the gen-AST (read-only tool; no codegen change) and MEASURE, for every shipped grammar: the
    fusible-rule set, the share of bench rule ENTRIES on fusible subtrees (regex: of the ~45-entries/`test` cascade), and the
    per-grammar ceiling. Output = the tool + a census table in this tree; the measured ceiling gates whether/where `.5.h.2+`
    (DFA emission increments) proceed — the `.5.d.4` STEP-0 discipline.
    **MEASURED VERDICT (the gate's answer): increment (i) token-RULE fusion is UNLANDABLE on the regex bench as scoped —
    conservative ceiling ≈1.04×** (absolute per-rule-fusion bound ≈1.8×); the regex per-char cost is dominated by the
    STRUCTURAL cascade (`piece`/`atom`/`quantifier`/`zero_width` probing ≈76% of bench entries), which only the
    increment-(ii) MERGED-CHOICE scanning shape can touch. SV/VHDL rule-fusion shares ≈0 (internal-layout criterion);
    their surface is the `match_regex` ATOM-site engine replacement (SV = 322 sites, census-measured — the self-hosting
    completion), which removes the external-engine dependency but eliminates no rule entries. **Road decision SURFACED
    to the director before any `.5.h.2` build** ([[feedback_stick_to_agreed_plan_no_silent_drift]]) — see section G below.

#### `.5.h.1` — STEP-0 FUSIBILITY CENSUS (session #106, 2026-07-13): tools + measurements + the gate verdict

**The two read-only tools built (no codegen change, no grammar change, parser-agnostic):**

1. **`ast_pipeline <g>.ebnf --report-fusibility-census`** (`rust/src/ast_pipeline/fusibility_census.rs` + the `main.rs`
   dispatch; optional `--fusibility-census-json FILE`, `--fusibility-entry-counts F1,F2,…`, `PGEN_FUSIBILITY_DUMP_ALL=1`
   for per-rule verdicts). Classifies every rule of the UNFILTERED tree (the view codegen compiles) against the section-F
   increment-1 gate, each criterion mirroring the engine's OWN resolution (never re-derived): (1) REGULAR — acyclic
   reference closure, quantifier-only iteration (`parse_quantifier_bounds`); (2) EFFECT-FREE — no compiled runtime
   directive anywhere in the closure (`compile_semantic_runtime_annotations` + `directives_for_rule`/branch maps), no
   value constraints, no `[> …]` follow restrictions, no mid-sequence inline actions, `@transform`/`@profiles` flagged
   as distinct increment-2 classes; (3) TEXT-FOLDING — the branch value is provably the matched span: unannotated
   terminal/alternation folds, `-> $text` (`UnifiedReturnAST::MatchedText` — value = span BY CONSTRUCTION, children need
   only shape), `-> $N` passthrough with all-lookahead siblings (the `unicode_char` shape); (4) POLICY-ENCODABLE —
   `longest_match`/`ordered`/`priority_first` all DFA-encodable (maximal munch / accept-priority), `nonassoc` +
   `@deterministic_group` excluded (registry accessors `effective_rule_*`); (5) LAYOUT-CONTIGUOUS — mirrors codegen
   exactly (`match_string` skips iff `!layout.terminals`; `match_regex` skips iff `!layout.regex_tokens` minus the two
   `string_content_*` name-special-cases): multi-lexeme bodies under a skipping facet are excluded. The classifier
   separates **shape-encodable** (value-agnostic language encodability — lookahead operands and `$text` subtrees need
   only this) from **fusible** (shape + value), so the report also measures the value-building-increment candidate pool
   (`shape_only`). Maximal fusible ROOTS (the future `scan_*` sites) = fusible rules referenced by ≥1 non-fusible rule.
   Also counts the grammar's regex-literal ATOM SITES (the `match_regex`/self-hosting surface). Conservative BY DESIGN —
   every verdict is a sound under-approximation, so the ceiling never over-promises. 4 unit tests pin the
   classifier's four load-bearing shapes (terminal-chain fusible + rooted-at-top, cycles excluded, lookahead-guarded
   `$N` passthrough → lookahead tier, quantified default fold not text).

2. **`parseability_probe --parse <g> <input> --dump-rule-entry-counts-json FILE`** (`parser_registry.rs` thread-local +
   `dump_rule_entry_counts_json` helper wired into all 11 grammars' detail-parse functions; worker-thread capture for
   regex; baseline-delta so SV's stdlib preload never pollutes the measured parse). Writes
   `{grammar, accepted, total_entries, rule_entry_counts}` from the always-on monotone per-rule entry counters
   (`fetch_add` at every rule entry, successful AND backtracked — `ast_based_generator.rs:3302`) — the machine-readable
   dual of the stderr live dashboard (`--dump-rule-call-counts`), which is refresh-based and useless for a
   sub-millisecond parse. Deterministic ⇒ a re-runnable oracle.

**Cross-validations (the tools agree with every prior independent measurement):**
- `test` = **50 raw rule entries** vs the `.5.e` figure **45**: reconciled exactly — `PGEN_REPORT_MEMO_STATS` reports
  45 = 24 success + 21 cached-failure MEMO TABLE entries (distinct outcomes); the counter counts raw entries including
  memo-hit re-entries (e.g. `atom` ×10). Same cascade, same ~11/char model; per-entry cost 11.79µs/50 ≈ 236ns raw.
- SV regex-literal atom sites = **322** measured vs the section-E tool-verified "~323 `match_regex` sites" (grep-based).
- regex atom sites = **0** — the REGEX-SELF-HOSTING conversion, reproduced.

**THE CENSUS (all shipped grammars; dynamic = entry counts on a real accepted parse — regex: the 8-pattern bench summed;
others: one representative corpus sample each, named in the method note below):**

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| regex | 274 | **68** (40+28) | 75 | 57 | 24.8% | 0 | 2389 (8-pattern bench) | 83 | 495 | 1811 (467) | **≈1.04×** |
| json | 9 | 2 | 0 | 2 | 22.2% | 14 | 24 | 0 | 8 | 16 (0) | ≈1.00× |
| ebnf | 131 | 14 | 36 | 13 | 10.7% | 30 | 3607 | 0 | 83 | 3524 (1663) | ≈1.00× |

Method notes: dynamic inputs = the 8 `regex_perf_probe` patterns (regex); `y_object.json` (json); `grammars/json.ebnf`
parsed by the ebnf parser; `entity_only_port_decl.vhd` (vhdl); `multiple_genvar_declarations.sv` @ `sv_2017` (SV);
`macro_define_undef_guard.sv` (svpp); `3 + 4 * WIDTH` (rtl_const_expr); a minimal assign module (rtl_frontend); a
`{type:…, atom:$1}` template (return_annotation); a real `@emit_fact` line (semantic_annotation). "eliminated" counts
entries on fusible NON-root rules only — a fusible rule referenced by ANY non-fusible parent is bucketed `at roots`
(per-rule aggregate counts cannot split call paths), so the ceiling is a sound LOWER bound; the absolute per-rule-fusion
UPPER bound (every fusible + shape-only entry absorbed) for regex is 2389/(2389−1045) ≈ **1.8×**. Ceiling model: uniform
per-entry cost (~236–262ns), scan cost ≈ a terminal match (first-order 0).

**WHY the estimate fell (the architectural reading, tool-backed):** section D's "~70–80% fused entries ⇒ ~3–5×" assumed
the per-char cascade is mostly token-shaped wrappers. The census shows the opposite: on the bench, only 24.2% of regex
entries sit on increment-1-fusible rules at all, and the eliminated share is 3.5% — the cascade's bulk is the
STRUCTURAL spine (`piece`→`atom` dispatch + the per-char failing probes of `quantifier`/`zero_width`/structural-`escape`
branches), each carrying a structural return annotation (`{type: "piece", …}`) that increment (i) by definition cannot
rebuild from a span. Those entries are exactly the increment-(ii) MERGED-CHOICE surface (one multi-accept DFA over a
choice site's token-shaped branch SUBSET acting as a prefix discriminator that also kills the failing probes) — a
different, site-level construction whose payoff needs its own STEP-0 measurement before any build.

#### G. ROAD DECISION SURFACED (director input REQUIRED before `.5.h.2` — [[feedback_stick_to_agreed_plan_no_silent_drift]])

The census (the agreed STEP-0 gate) answers the gate question AGAINST the road's regex-payoff premise: **increment (i)
token-rule fusion cannot clear the ⛔ land-iff-faster bar on the regex bench (≈1.04× conservative / ≈1.8× absolute
per-rule bound), and SV/VHDL rule-fusion shares are ≈0.** What remains REAL, measured: (a) the **`match_regex`
ATOM-site engine replacement** — SV 322 / semantic_annotation 136 / vhdl 102 / rtl_frontend 47 / ebnf+svpp 30 each —
the self-hosting completion (removes the Rust-regex dependency platform-wide; per-call constant win; no entry
elimination, so NOT a bench multiplier); (b) the **increment-(ii) merged-choice surface** — regex's structural
per-char probing spine (≈76% of bench entries), NOT measured by this rule-level census. Single recommendation
(engineer): extend STEP-0 with a read-only **choice-SITE census** (`.5.h.1b`: for every Or site, the token-shaped
branch subset + the bench entry share its DFA pre-discrimination would kill) BEFORE agreeing to build anything —
same measure-first discipline, one more cheap analysis pass; then re-adjudicate the rung (build increment (ii) /
re-scope to self-hosting-only / park) on that number. Awaiting the director's pick.

> **Session #107 note (PNT, within-road):** the pick the director owns is the `.5.h.2+` BUILD decision. The
> `.5.h.1b` census itself is read-only, forecloses nothing, and is exactly the agreed STEP-0 measure-first
> discipline extended to the increment-(ii) surface — so PNT proceeds with it, and the section-G re-adjudication
> will be put to the director ON the measured number. No build starts without the explicit pick.

#### G.1 RE-ADJUDICATION ON THE MEASURED NUMBERS (session #107 — BOTH increments now measured; director pick pending)

The `.5.h.1b` census (results section below) closes the open measurement from section G. The complete picture:

| increment | surface | measured ceiling (8-pattern bench) |
|---|---|---|
| (i) token-RULE fusion | fusible-rule descents | ≈1.04× conservative (`.5.h.1`) |
| (ii) MERGED-CHOICE / site-DFA discrimination | discarded entries on shape-encodable rules (830 of 2389) | **≈1.53×** (`.5.h.1b`; optimistic — memo-hit entries at full weight, scans costed ~0) |
| (i)+(ii) combined ABSOLUTE bound | every encodable entry absorbed (1045 of 2389) | ≈1.78× (cross-instrument-reproduced) |
| what remains | the STRUCTURAL spine: 402 committed + 943 discarded structural entries = 56% of the bench | out of scanner-rung scope (recursive descent itself) |

**Even perfect execution of the ENTIRE agreed scanner-rung scope cannot reach 2× on the regex bench** — against
`.5.h`'s original ~3–5× estimate and a residual ≈20× architectural gap vs PCRE2 (`.5.e`). The probing waste IS
huge (74% of entries), but the killable-by-DFA share is the minority of it; the majority sits on the recursive
structural rules a DFA cannot decide exactly.

**Single recommendation (engineer): RE-SCOPE the rung to SELF-HOSTING-ONLY** — the `match_regex` ATOM-site
engine replacement (SV 322 / semantic_annotation 136 / vhdl 102 / rtl_frontend 47 / ebnf+svpp 30 each): a
platform-wide dependency-removal + self-hosting-doctrine deliverable with a per-call constant win, sequenced at
the director's priority — and CLOSE the rung's regex-bench SPEED claim as **REFUTED BY MEASUREMENT** (both
increments). The next regex-bench speed rung candidate is `.7` (batch + parse-cache), already agreed-sequenced
after `.5`. Alternative on the table: park the rung entirely. Awaiting the director's pick; no `.5.h.2` build
starts without it.

**RESOLUTION (director, 2026-07-13, session #107 — see section H):** the road forward is the
OPTIMIZING-COMPILER rung (`.5.i`). `.5.h` is **PARKED**: its regex-bench speed claim stays closed
(measurement-refuted); the `match_regex` self-hosting surface remains a REAL, censused, orthogonal deliverable
(dependency removal + self-hosting-doctrine completion) available for explicit prioritization later — no
autonomous re-open.

#### H. THE AGREED ROAD #2 (director, 2026-07-13, session #107) — the OPTIMIZING-COMPILER ("planner") rung; `.5.i` OPENED

The post-refutation discussion converged on the architectural diagnosis and the director agreed explicitly:
*"I agree with your phrasing — in fact we just implemented 2/3 of the journey; the last is to effectively build
a compiler or planner, because what we have right now is a naive version, a transliterator. That's where things
are going to be interesting."*

**The division-of-labor contract (the agreed framing):**
- A spec language SHOULD NOT capture speed (SQL/planner, regex/engine precedents): the EBNF+annotations capture
  FUNCTIONALITY (accuracy) — they are and remain the sole semantic source of truth, byte-for-byte.
- Speed is MANUFACTURED by the compiler from what the spec already implies. PGEN today has 2/3 of the system at
  full strength: the SPEC half (EBNF+annotations) and the PROOF half (typed-AST byte-identity, cert seeds,
  equivalence gates, the interpreter as unoptimized reference). The COMPILER half is a TRANSLITERATOR — it maps
  every construct 1:1 onto maximally-defensive generic machinery (rule→guarded/memoized method, `|`→full
  tournament, `?`/`*`→snapshot-speculation) regardless of what the grammar needs at that site. That protocol is
  the measured ~236ns/entry × ~11 entries/char; 74% of bench entries are its discarded probes.
- **Hints doctrine:** the compiler DERIVES all speed decisions from grammar analysis (FIRST sets, disjointness,
  call graphs, store-gate placement). Grammar authors never think about speed. If advisory hints ever exist they
  must be provably acceptance/AST-neutral — the EBNF never becomes an implementation script.

        longest-match / priority_first / ordered / associativity all degenerate to the same sole candidate;

    (e) the rule has NO Branch-phase predicates and NO branch-start effect directives (a predicate-rejected

    candidate branch, still run under `try_parse` (the speculation wrapper keeps the snapshot/restore contract
    [[feedback_try_parse_must_snapshot_semantic_state]] for the failure arm); on success the branch's semantic
    effects STAY in place (no losers ran; gate (e) excludes predicate rejection) — the tournament-scope
    checkpoint clone, `extract_delta_since` + C3-B `rollback_to_labeled` + winner `apply_delta` round-trip,
    the `should_take` cascade, `best_*` bookkeeping, and the partition-offset computation are ALL elided
    (rotation only permutes evaluation order, which is irrelevant when at most one branch can match);
    the branch transform is evaluated at candidate end (the BRANCH-BROADCAST-FIX.3 order is trivially
    preserved — nothing rolls back); no-candidate byte / EOF / branch failure ⇒ the byte-identical existing

    Goal: **P1 —
    CASCADE/WRAPPER INLINING** —
    collapse wrapper-frame rule calls into their call sites so the hot descent
    (`piece→atom→literal→literal_char→letter` = 5 full guarded/memoized frames per literal char) stops paying
    the per-frame protocol where analysis proves it redundant; pure protocol removal, parse semantics
    identical by construction. The `.5.i.1` census measured the surface: **~290 of 617 committed bench
    entries are wrapper frames** (letter 47, literal 34, literal_char 34, escape chain ~90, entry_* 24…),
    pre-P0 ceiling ≈−12%; post-P0/P2 the per-frame elidable protocol is smaller — the STEP-0 scout re-prices
    it honestly.
    **The per-frame protocol (WHY+WHERE, read from the regenerated `regex_parser.rs`, e.g. `parse_literal`):**
    every rule entry pays (1) recursion-guard enter/exit (+ `check_cycle` scan on recursive rules); (2) the
    memo probes (fail-set / tainted-map / get — the `.5.i.1` ≈17–22% bucket) and, on each of the bench's 661
    body-execution successes, the memo success-insert (`node.clone()` + delta extraction); (3) the
    `needs_raw_post/final` annotation-table probes; (4) rule-context push/pop + the `trace_rules` scope probe;
    (5) the call frame itself, nested inside the caller's per-branch `try_parse`. For a wrapper frame (a
    bare-rule-ref branch like `atom = literal | …` delegating to a cheap child) this protocol IS the cost.
    **Rule-eligibility gates (static, at codegen; ALL required; the scout encodes them and NAMES the failing
    gate per rule):**
    (a) the rule participates in NO call-graph cycle (its recursion guard is then provably non-load-bearing;
        rules the inlined body references keep their own frames unless independently eligible);
    (b) the rule carries NO semantic directives in ANY phase (no pre/branch/post/final predicates, no
        `@emit_fact`/`@open_scope`/`@close_scope`, no branch-start effects, no `value_compare` rule-span
        constraint, no `@import_from_library`) — the frame's transactional/context role is then trace-naming
        only;
    (c) the rule is not the entry rule and not an `--entry-rule` alternate start (the rule METHOD stays
        emitted regardless — inlining changes CALL SITES only, so entry-relative parsing and any ineligible
        caller keep the method);
    (d) no `@profiles` divergence between the rule and the call site (the inlined copy must resolve
        identically under every declared profile).
    **Two priced increments (scope decision taken on the scout's numbers, not intuition):**
    - **P1a — frame collapse, memo PRESERVED:** the child body emitted inline inside the caller's branch
      `try_parse`, still under `memoized_call(RULE_<CHILD>, …)`, with the child's observability side-effects
      verbatim (rule-entry `fetch_add`, coverage push, furthest-position, the trace-gated 🚪/✅/❌/🏁 lines).
      Elides guard + context push/pop + `needs_raw` probes (gate (b) ⇒ statically false) + the call frame.
      Counters/committed dumps/cert pins stay BYTE-IDENTICAL by construction (memo hit-replay semantics
      unchanged).
    - **P1b — memo elision at inlined frames:** additionally drops the memo probes + success-insert clone.
      Result-neutral BY CONSTRUCTION (the memo is a pure cache; regex has no predicates ⇒ no taint), but a
      former memo HIT becomes a body re-execution ⇒ the child-subtree entry counters/committed dumps change
      where hits occurred (counters stay TRUTHFUL — they count real executions — but not byte-identical to
      pre-P1), and re-execution time replaces hit-replay-clone time. The scout's per-rule memo-hit
      decomposition prices this: for terminal-leaf bodies (post-P2 byte-switches) re-execution may be CHEAPER
      than the hit-replay clone — measure, don't assume. If P1b lands, the counter delta is a DOCUMENTED
      observability delta (the P2 C3-B precedent); cert-coverage pins must still hold (witnessing walks the
      parse tree, which is byte-identical).
    **Observability position (recorded up front):** the typed/raw AST is byte-identical (the inlined body
    constructs the child node exactly as the rule method would, rule_name included); 🚪/✅/❌/🏁 lines
    preserved at inlined frames; rollback labels INSIDE an inlined subtree name the parent frame
    (`parse_stack` has no child entry — a documented trace-payload delta, V2-class, trace-only); always-on
    counters/coverage/furthest preserved under P1a, P1b as adjudicated above.
    **STEP-0 SCOUT FIRST (tools-first; a fabricated ceiling dies here like the scanner's did):** extend the
    fusibility census with an **INLINE-CENSUS**: per rule — the eligibility verdict + NAMED blockers + wrapper
    class (pass-through `-> $1` / bare-ref alternation leaf / shaped) + static reference-site count (code-size
    duplication estimate); joined with the outcome dump ⇒ raw/committed/discarded bench entries on eligible
    rules; plus a NEW read-only **per-rule memo-HIT decomposition** (hits = raw entries − body executions,
    surfaced per rule) ⇒ the P1b lost-hit surface. Output = the measured frame exposure + a falsifiable
    ceiling PER INCREMENT (P1a / P1b) priced at the post-P0/P2 per-frame protocol cost minus the modeled
    lost-hit re-execution cost, recorded BEFORE any emission code. NOTE (recorded at open): P1 changes the
    site mix ⇒ re-run the degeneracy census after landing (P2 exposure may grow — new top-level shapes appear
    when wrappers collapse).
    ⛔ HARD CONSTRAINT unchanged: land iff measured faster (fat-LTO alternated 5×2000, geomean-of-mins) +

    duality-hunt; PCRE2 compile oracle; clippy strict-source; all parsers regen with the non-regex
    byte-identity audit); count-dump identity per the P1a/P1b adjudication above; book/tree/CHANGES/MEMORY

#### `.5.i.5` STEP-0 — METHOD-FRAME MACHINERY CENSUS RESULTS (session #113, 2026-07-13): P3a ≈−2% (thin), P3b ≈−4–8%; measured entirely from EXISTING instruments — no new code needed

**Method (tools-first, zero new instrument):** the post-P1b outcome dumps (per-rule
entries/hits — the `.5.i.4` counter), the census JSON (per-rule blockers incl. the cycle
blocker, `decided`, degeneracy classes), and the structural fact that a DECIDED rule's
`rule_reference` sites are ALL inlined ⇒ its method entries ≈ 0 on the bench (entry-relative
dispatch only) ⇒ method-frame entries = entries on NON-decided rules. Cross-check: decided
1223 + method 1280 = 2503 total ✓.

**THE MEASURED P3 SURFACE (8-pattern bench, P1b parsers, ≈330–340µs bench sum):**
- Method-frame entries **1280 (51.1%)** — **796 on ACYCLIC method rules** (the P3a
  guard-elision surface) vs 484 on CYCLIC rules (guard load-bearing, stays).
- All **314 residual memo hits** sit at method frames: **174 acyclic / 140 cyclic** — the 140
  cyclic-spine hits are UNTOUCHABLE (the P3b hard bound: cycle rules keep the memo).
- **P3b candidates (acyclic + directive-free method rules): 84 of 146 method rules — 570
  entries, 144 addressable hits** (top: `class_literal` 71h/97e, `digit` 25h/40e, `letter`
  15h/57e); 19 of the 84 are P2-degenerate byte-switch leaves (130 entries / 47 hits) where
  re-execution is most plausibly cheaper than replay.
- **HONEST CEILINGS (falsifiable, recorded before any emission; `.5.i.1` cost basis):**
  - **P3a** — guard enter/exit ≈9ns × 796 acyclic method entries ≈ **7µs ⇒ ≈−2%**. ⚠️ BELOW
    the reliable per-session signal threshold alone (per-round geomean noise is ±1–3%) —
    measure COMBINED with P3b (one candidate parser carrying both elisions); attribute P3a
    separately only if the combined lands and attribution is worth a second build pair.
  - **P3b** — probes ≈10–14ns × 570 ≈ 6–8µs + inserts ≈68–103ns × ≈200–230 candidate successes
    ≈ 14–24µs − lost-hit re-execution (144 × ≈0–40ns) ≈ 0–6µs ⇒ **net ≈ 14–26µs ⇒ ≈−4–8%**.
  - **P3c** — not yet classified (Optim #14 already elides the semantic-transaction wrapper on
    annotation-free rules; P2 elided tournament protocol at degenerate sites; the residual
    wrapper surface needs a profile, below).
- **RECOMMENDED NEXT ACTION (the `.2` profiling discipline, BEFORE P3 emission):** re-profile
  the ≈32µs bench (macOS `sample`, release probe) — after P0/P2/P1a/P1b the per-frame protocol
  is heavily harvested, and the residual time may now be dominated by terminal matching, node

  profile decides whether P3a+P3b (combined ceiling ≈−6–10%) or P4 value folding is the higher

  scout protocol exists to kill.

#### `.5.i.5` RE-PROFILE #5 (session #114, 2026-07-13): P3a+P3b REFUTED BY PROFILE (guard 0.4–0.5%, ENTIRE memo 2.8–3.2%) — the residual is VALUE/ALLOC-dominated: serde output ≈26–29% (P4), C3-B tournament delta protocol ≈8–10% (P3c), alloc-family leaves ≈52%

release (fat-LTO) from HEAD against the on-disk P1b parsers (regex `009e1485…`): the binary
reproduced **byte-identical to the session-#113 measured P1b candidate** (sha256 `0ee787e1…`) —
zero build drift by construction. Drift-control sanity PASSED: geomean-of-mins **33.70µs /
32.49µs** over two 2000-sample rounds (pinned P1b era ≈32µs, best-mins 31.53µs — reproduces
within the ±1–3% round noise). Then TWO macOS `sample` windows, 30s @ 1ms each, over
`--samples 100000` steady-state runs (the probe is SEQUENTIAL per pattern, so one 30s window
cannot cover the ~40s 8-pattern pass — a coverage hole named, not silently accepted): window A
from launch (patterns 1–7, **25,096** root samples) + window B attached at +12s (the heavy
fact-emitting tail including the FULL `anchor_complex` segment, **22,820** root samples); 99.5%
of samples inside `time_one_parse`; the union covers all 8 patterns and the two windows agree on
every headline bucket ⇒ the verdict is STABLE. Raw: session scratchpad `sample_reprofile5.txt` /
`sample_reprofile5b.txt` + the RE-PROFILE-#4 `analyze_sample.py` (self-time per frame; allocation
leaves attributed to the nearest classified ancestor).

**THE MEASURED ≈32µs PROFILE (bucket totals = direct self + inherited + attributed allocs; window A / window B):**

| bucket | A | B (heavy tail) | reading |

**VERDICTS (the STEP-0 ceilings adjudicated — the scout protocol did exactly its job):**
- ⛔ **P3a REFUTED as an increment** — the profiler bounds the TOTAL recursion-guard cost
  (cyclic frames included) at **0.4–0.5%** of wall time; the acyclic-method-addressable share is
  a fraction of that. Unmeasurable under the ±1–3% per-round noise floor. NO emission.
- ⛔ **P3b REFUTED as an increment** — the census cost model (probes ≈10–14ns + inserts
  ≈68–103ns ⇒ ≈−4–8%) is a STALE-COST-BASIS estimate at the ≈32µs bench: the profiler bounds the
  **ENTIRE memo machinery** — all probes, all inserts, hit replay, and its allocation traffic,
  cyclic rules included — at **2.8–3.2%**. The P3b-addressable acyclic-method slice is a strict
  fraction (the cyclic spine KEEPS the memo, hard bound) ⇒ realistic ceiling ≲−1.5–2%, sub-noise.
  NO emission. This is precisely the "P3 emission on a stale cost model = the fabricated-ceiling
  class" the leaf's re-profile step existed to kill — it killed it BEFORE any code was written.
- ✅ **P3c CONFIRMED with a real, named surface ≈−8–10%**: `rollback_to_labeled` is the TOP named
  PGEN leaf symbol of the whole profile (718 top-of-stack samples, window A), plus
  `extract_delta_since` + `SemanticRuntimeDelta` build/drop + `SemanticFactRecord`/`ScopeNode`/
  `DeferredObligation` drops. WHERE (engine grep, `generated/regex_parser.rs`): EVERY generated
  `extract_delta_since`/`rollback_to_labeled` call site sits at a `tournament_semantic_checkpoint`
  — the C3-B tournament branch protocol (each successful branch's store delta extracted, state
  rolled back so branches compete from identical state, the winner's delta re-applied), paid on
  EVERY multi-branch Or body with a successful branch REGARDLESS of store activity (6/8 bench
  patterns emit zero facts; the protocol runs anyway), plus the residual per-rule
  `with_semantic_runtime_rule_transaction` wrapper on directive-carrying rules. Elision gates for
  the P3c scout to price: (i) STATIC — subtree store-effect-freedom via the census's existing
  effect-freedom criterion (⛔ deferred final-phase obligations COUNT as store effects); (ii)
  DYNAMIC — an O(1) store write-epoch fast path (epoch unchanged since checkpoint ⇒ the delta is
  provably empty ⇒ skip extract/rollback/re-apply; the MEMO-STORE-SOUNDNESS.2 epoch machinery is
  the precedent). P2's degenerate sites already elide this protocol — P3c generalizes the elision
  to the non-degenerate sites.
- ✅ **P4 CONFIRMED as the dominant addressable surface ≈26–29% + adjacents**: named mechanisms —
  `ParseContent::to_json_value` re-CLONES `Value` subtrees at the output boundary (BTreeMap
  `clone_subtree` nearest-ancestor join: **1,189** of ~4,400 clone samples under `to_json_value`,
  the single largest site); in-parse `Value` clones under the `parse_regex`/`parse_piece`/
  `parse_atom` closures (return-annotation shaped-view captures); `drop_in_place<serde_json::Value>`
  churn; and `split_semantic_top_level` — the value-constraint expression RE-PARSING the `.5.i.1`
  census priced at 2–4% — now a NAMED leaf symbol (474 top-of-stack samples ≈1.9%, P4's
  expression pre-compilation component). Adjacent surface: vec_clone 4.6–5.2% + node_build 2.7% +
  the transient-alloc share of rule_methods. **Alloc-family leaves total ≈51.6% of ALL samples**
  (window A: `_xzm_free` 5,567 + mallocs ≈2,800 + `memmove` 1,301 + `free` 934 + bzero/memset
  ≈960 + …) — half the parse is still allocator traffic post-arena, and it is now
  `Value`/owned-clone traffic, NOT `ParseNode` traffic.

**⇒ STEER (within the fixed planner order P0→P2→P1→P3→P4 — no deviation):** P3 proceeds as
**P3c ONLY** — next work unit = the P3c pricing scout (classify tournament sites by
store-effect-freedom, join with the outcome dumps for exposure, price gates (i)/(ii), record a
falsifiable ceiling BEFORE any emission). P3a/P3b are CLOSED refuted-by-measurement (no emission,
nothing to revert). P4 (value folding) follows as the pass with the largest measured surface.

#### `.5.i.5` P3c PRICING SCOUT (session #114, 2026-07-13): 98.8% of rollbacks and 93.6% of delta extractions are PROVABLY-UNCHANGED no-ops; the priced increment = the ENGINE-ONLY epoch fast path, falsifiable ceiling ≈−5–8%

**The instrument (landed this slice — observability tier, lib-only, NO regen, NO codegen change):**
four cumulative counters in `SemanticStoreCounters`, recorded by `rollback_to_labeled` from
values it ALREADY computes (the hoisted write-epoch guard verdict + one O(1) `deferred_len`
compare + a structural `RollbackLabel` match — nothing materialized, zero new heavy work):
`rollbacks_unchanged` (store provably unchanged since checkpoint: epoch-guard false AND
deferred-obligation length unchanged — the O(1)-fast-path-eligible population),
`rollbacks_tournament` (`C3bBranchCleanup` labels — 1:1 with `extract_delta_since` calls),
`rollbacks_tournament_unchanged` (empty-delta extractions), `rollbacks_nonempty_chain`
(checkpoints that paid a real chain-clone alloc). Dumped through the existing `store_counters`
surface (`--dump-rule-outcome-counts-json`, both the macro path and the regex worker path; old
dumps stay loadable — the census never reads `store_counters`). Unit test
`rollback_classification_counters_track_protocol_exposure` pins all four classifications
INCLUDING the epoch-blind deferred-obligation case (the soundness pin for any O(1) gate).

**THE MEASURED EXPOSURE (8-pattern bench, P1b parsers; per-pattern dumps in the session
scratchpad `p3c_dumps/`; instrument cross-check: totals reproduce the P1b pins BYTE-EXACTLY —
entries 2503 / committed 617 / hits 314; dump re-run `cmp`-identical):**

| pattern | rollbacks | unchanged | tournament (=extracts) | tour-unchanged (=empty deltas) | facts |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| literal_simple | 41 | 41 | 17 | 17 | 0 |
| digit_sequence | 272 | 272 | 30 | 30 | 0 |
| character_class | 667 | 667 | 162 | 162 | 0 |
| alternation | 101 | 101 | 41 | 41 | 0 |
| capture_groups | 627 | 608 | 48 | 32 | 24 |
| url_simple | 122 | 122 | 37 | 37 | 0 |
| email_basic | 299 | 299 | 25 | 25 | 0 |
| anchor_complex | 604 | 591 | 64 | 53 | 24 |
| **Σ** | **2733** | **2701 (98.8%)** | **424** | **397 (93.6%)** | 48 |

`rollbacks_nonempty_chain` = 2733/2733 (**100%**): `SemanticRuntimeState::new()` seeds
`active_chain = vec![ScopeId::ROOT]`, so EVERY `checkpoint()` clones a non-empty Vec (an
alloc+drop per speculation — ≥2733 per bench pass, plus the checkpoints of SUCCESSFUL
speculations this rollback-side counter cannot see: a lower bound). `scopes_opened` delta = 0 on
all 8 patterns — no bench pattern opens a scope; the chain is always the root alone.

**WHY+WHERE (engine reading, `semantic_runtime.rs`):** on an unchanged store the protocol still
pays — `checkpoint()` :2728 clones the chain unconditionally; `extract_delta_since` :2783-2788
UNCONDITIONALLY clones `final_active_chain` + `final_scopes` (plus 4 empty subslice `to_vec`s +
the `contains` filter) even for a delta that is empty by construction, then the
`SemanticRuntimeDelta` is dropped; `rollback_to_labeled` compares the chain against the snapshot
TWICE (the epoch guard + `scope_state_changed`) and walks the empty fact range. The `.3`
rollback-gate (session #90) already elides the expensive scope RESTORE on the unchanged path —
this is the residue it left, now the top named leaf (`rollback_to_labeled` 718 samples).

**THE PRICED INCREMENT — P3c-i "epoch fast path" (ENGINE-only, parser-agnostic, no regen, the
interpreter inherits it automatically):**
- `checkpoint()` additionally stamps `write_epoch` (one u64 — O(1)).
- `extract_delta_since`: `epoch unchanged && deferred_len unchanged` ⇒ return the canonical
  EMPTY delta — skip both clones, the subslice copies, and the filter. SOUND: every store
  mutation bumps the epoch (audited this slice: `open_scope`/`close_scope`/`emit_fact`/
  `push_fact_record`/`apply_delta`-on-change/`rollback`-on-change); deferred obligations are the
  ONE epoch-blind mutation (deliberate — they don't taint the memo) ⇒ the explicit length check;
  an empty delta's `final_*` fields are NEVER consumed (codegen skips `apply_delta` via the
  existing `is_empty()` guard, which ignores `final_*` by design).
- `rollback_to_labeled`: same O(1) predicate up front ⇒ skip the fact-index walk, the
  truncations, and the SECOND chain compare; all counters stay (oracle surface).
- **Falsifiable ceiling ≈ −5–8%**: savings population = 397 empty extracts × ≈25–50ns (2 clones
  + empty builds + filter + delta drop) + 2701 no-op rollbacks × ≈3–8ns (compare/branch residue),
  raw ≈ 18–42µs, CAPPED by the measured profile bucket (snapshot_rollback 8.4–10.0% ≈ 28–34µs of
  the ≈330–340µs bench sum) minus the changed-population share and the O(1) residue.
- ⛔ Emission-slice soundness audit (required): enumerate EVERY `&mut self` method that touches
  `facts`/`scope_arena`/`active_chain`/`scopes`/`deferred_obligations` and prove each bumps the
  epoch or is delta-invisible (`promote_facts_since` is currently a parameter-ignoring no-op —
  re-verify at emission).
- **NOT addressed by P3c-i (recorded, no silent scope-shrink):** the `checkpoint()`-side chain
  clone (≥2733 allocs/pass, profiled inside `semantic_other` ≈6%) — needs a STATIC
  effect-free-site elision (P3c-ii: census transitive store-effect-freedom + codegen skips the
  whole protocol at qualifying sites) or an undo-journal chain redesign (P3c-iii); re-price on a
  fresh profile AFTER P3c-i lands.

#### `.5.i.5` P3c-i LANDED (session #114, 2026-07-13): THE EPOCH FAST PATH — regex geomean **−11.2%** (all 5 rounds faster, ALL 8 patterns faster at best-mins), ≈35µs-era → ≈31µs, cumulative 496µs → ≈31µs ≈ 16×

**THE CHANGE (ENGINE-only, `semantic_runtime.rs` — parser-agnostic; NO codegen change, NO regen;
the interpreter inherits it automatically):** `SemanticRuntimeCheckpoint` gains a `write_epoch`
stamp (one u64, recorded by `checkpoint()`); `extract_delta_since` and `rollback_to_labeled`
each get the O(1) fast path `epoch unchanged && deferred_len unchanged` ⇒ (extract) return the
canonical EMPTY delta — skipping the two unconditional clones, the subslice copies, and the
closed-scope filter; (rollback) skip the fact-index walk, truncations, and both chain compares —
counters recorded exactly as the slow path would (`rollbacks_unchanged` by construction).
Soundness: the mutation-site AUDIT (scout section above) — every delta-visible mutation bumps
the monotone epoch except the deliberately epoch-blind obligation enqueue, covered by the
explicit length compare; an empty delta's `final_*` fields are NEVER consumed (all 4

#### `.5.i.5` P4 STEP-0 PRICING SCOUT (session #115, 2026-07-14): the `split_semantic_top_level` 6–8% is 18 evaluations/pass of GRAMMAR-CONSTANT PROSE `@constraint` strings (≈0.7–1.4µs EACH) — P4-i = codegen CONSTANT-FOLD, ceiling ≈−5–8%; P4-ii/iii/iv priced from the #6 profile

**Method (tools-first, ZERO new instrument):** generated-parser + codegen source reading joined
with the EXISTING session-#114 outcome dumps (`p3c_dumps`/`p3ci_dumps` per-rule
raw/committed/hits + `store_counters`) and the RE-PROFILE #6 inclusive-subtree numbers.

**P4-i — `@constraint` CONSTANT-FOLD AT CODEGEN (the top-named-leaf mechanism, fully mapped):**
- **WHY+WHERE (emission):** `ast_based_generator.rs` `semantic_relational_constraint_tokens`
  (:8699, policy :8655) emits, at EVERY exit of a `@constraint`-carrying rule,
  `evaluate_relational_expression(&result, "<grammar-constant string>")` + an error branch.
  The emitted evaluator RE-PARSES the string per evaluation: trim → paren-strip walk → `||`
  split (byte-walk + Vec alloc) → `&&` split (walk + alloc) → up to SIX operator once-splits
  (each a FULL walk + Vec alloc) → reference/unquote/f64 probes → `to_ascii_lowercase`
  (String alloc) → truthy fallback ⇒ ≈9 byte-walks + ≈9 allocs to conclude "prose ⇒ true".
- **THE POPULATION (dump join, per bench pass):** **18 raw evaluations** — ALL on
  `simple_escape` (email 6, anchor_complex 4, digit_sequence 3, capture_groups 3,
  character_class 1, url_simple 1; committed 16/18, hits 0). The other generated sites
  (`control_escape` ×6, `single_byte_escape` ×2, `unicode_escape`, `property_escape`,
  hex/octal/codepoint) are IDLE on this bench. Profile cap: split-subtree INCLUSIVE 6.3/8.2%
  (A/B) ⇒ measured per-eval cost ≈0.7–1.4µs — each evaluation costs ≈¼ of a whole
  `literal_simple` parse.
- **THE FOLD (all 15 sites provably constant-TRUE):** every one of the 8 distinct grammar
  strings (`grammars/regex.ebnf` `@constraint:` prose, e.g. "produces control character") is
  operator-free, `||`/`&&`-free, un-negated, NOT reference syntax (no `$`), unquoted,
  non-numeric, ∉ {true,false} ⇒ the emitted decision procedure lands in
  `semantic_truthy(non-empty prose) = true` UNCONDITIONALLY; all 15 sites carry EMPTY
  `requires` refs (`&[]`, verified 15/15); `@implies` is used NOWHERE. Codegen therefore
  classifies the constant at GENERATION time (the narrowest sound gate: no top-level
  structure ∧ not reference/quoted/numeric/boolean ⇒ constant-true) and **emits NOTHING** —
  the runtime check is dead code by construction (a constant-true check has no observable
  surface: no trace line, no store touch, an error branch that can never fire). Non-constant
  expressions keep today's runtime path unchanged. Parser-agnostic: the fold lives in the
  SHARED codegen path (any grammar's prose `@constraint` benefits; regex is today's only live
  user — semantic_annotation's one `@constraint` match is a comment).
- **FALSIFIABLE CEILING ≈ −5–8% geomean** — cap = the whole measured mechanism (6.3–8.2%
  inclusive + the small evaluator tail outside split frames); population-derived cross-check
  18 × 0.7–1.4µs ≈ 13–25µs of the ≈300µs bench sum ✓ consistent. Skew: email/anchor/digit/
  capture faster; literal/alternation untouched (0 evaluations).
- **Honest bound (recorded, out of increment scope):** the INTERPRETER does not evaluate
  `@constraint` at all (no relational machinery in `parse_harness_interpreter.rs`) — a
  PRE-EXISTING latent divergence class for a hypothetical constant-FALSE or content-dependent
  constraint (today's equivalence-gate green + all-prose-true constraints make it invisible).
  The fold does not widen it: a folded constant-true check is behaviorally identical on both
  sides. A durable closure (interpreter mirror or a constant-false lint) is a separate leaf.
- ⛔ Emission-slice requirements: the `.5.i.4` battery verbatim (land-iff-faster alternated
  5×2000 + byte-identical full oracle set + all-11 regen with per-parser diff audit + the
  dual-feature `ast_pipeline` rebuild trap).

**P4-ii — memo-stored `Value` ownership (drop-churn):** cap = `drop<RegexParser>` 5.4–5.5%
(parser teardown cascading the memo's STORED shaped `Value`s) + in-parse `MemoEntry` drops
≈1.0–1.5%. Design: stop cloning shaped values into memo entries (Rc/arena ownership across
replay). **Ceiling ≈ −4–6%; needs a design spike** (replay ownership semantics).

**P4-iii — shaped-view capture clone folding:** cap = `ParseContent::clone` inclusive
11.3–11.7% (+ `clone_subtree` overlap 12.7–14.1%). CLASS-WIDE — spread across the
`parse_piece`/`parse_atom`/`parse_regex`/`parse_entry_alternation` shaped-view capture
closures with NO dominant site ⇒ requires the value-representation design (Rc<Value>/CoW/
arena-Json), not a point fix. **The LARGEST P4 surface: ceiling ≈ −9–13%.**

**P4-iv — boundary `to_json_value` move-not-clone:** cap = inclusive 3.8–4.1% ⇒ **ceiling
≈ −3%** (the #5 "largest single site" framing is dead — RE-PROFILE #6).

**⇒ STEER (emission order within P4):** **P4-i FIRST** (clean, codegen-only, sound gate fully
verified this scout, ceiling ≈−5–8%) → re-profile → **P4-iii design spike** (largest surface)
with **P4-ii folded into the same value-ownership design**; P4-iv rides along on that design.

#### `.5.i.6` P4-i LANDED (session #115, 2026-07-14): the `@constraint` CONSTANT-FOLD — regex geomean **−12.1%** (all 5 rounds faster), ≈31µs-era → **≈27.4µs**, cumulative 496µs → ≈27.4µs ≈ **18.1×**

**THE CHANGE (codegen-only, parser-agnostic — `ast_based_generator.rs`; NO engine change, NO
grammar change):** `semantic_relational_constraint_tokens` now consults a generation-time
constant-truth gate `relational_constraint_is_provably_truthy(expr)` and, when the rule's whole
relational policy is exactly {constant-true `@constraint`, zero `@requires` refs, no `@implies`},
**emits NOTHING** — the rule-exit guard is dead code by construction (no trace line, no store
effect, an error branch that can never fire; `enforce_relational_requires(&[])` proven a pure
no-op). The gate is STRICTLY narrower than the emitted evaluator, each banned character mapping
to one evaluator feature (doc-comment carries the full mirror map): non-empty ∧ none of
`$ " ' ( ) < > = ! | &` ∧ **not a bare dotted-identifier chain** ∧ `f64` parse fails ∧ lowered ∉
{true,false,0,no,off,none,null}. Non-constant expressions keep the runtime path VERBATIM.
**Soundness near-miss caught by the full-procedure read (recorded):** `semantic_reference_syntax`
accepts `$`-less bare/dotted identifiers (`validated`, `a.b.c`) — such a "constraint" resolves
against parse content and can REJECT when unresolved, so a naive prose gate keyed only on
banned characters would have mis-folded it; the identifier-chain exclusion (mirroring
`semantic_identifier` byte-for-byte) closes it, and the truth-table test pins both directions.
Unit tests: `relational_constraint_constant_truth_gate` (8 live strings fold; 30 adversarial
shapes don't) + `constant_true_constraint_folds_to_no_emission` (emission-level pin, both
directions).

**MEASURE (decisive, the ⛔ protocol — alternated 5×2000, geomean-of-mins):** baseline = the
saved landed P3c-i probe `09b20e98…`; candidate = fresh fat-LTO build on the folded regen
`f5506c88…`. Per-round ratios **0.884/0.887/0.877/0.879/0.868** ⇒ geomean **0.879 = −12.1%**,
candidate faster EVERY round; best-mins 31.30µs → **27.38µs** (0.875). Per-pattern best-min
ratios MATCH the scout's population map: email_basic **0.690** (6 evals), digit_sequence
**0.737** (3 evals, small parse), url_simple 0.849, anchor_complex 0.888, capture_groups 0.921,
character_class 0.962 (1 eval, large parse); the two ZERO-evaluation patterns: alternation 0.988
(noise) and literal_simple **1.022** — the fold removes no code on literal_simple's path, so the
+2.2% (~110ns on the 5µs pattern) is fat-LTO layout jitter, the known smallest-pattern class;
geomean faster all rounds regardless. EXCEEDS the ≈−5–8% ceiling again — the ceiling capped
only the split-subtree share; the evaluator tail (lowercase/truthy/error-format machinery),
≈9-allocs/eval allocator relief (V6-class synergy), and the I-cache effect of deleting 15
format!-heavy sites ride on top. Cumulative: 496µs → ≈27.4µs ≈ **18.1×**.

**Battery (all green, pre-landing):**
- **Regen diff audit:** all-11 canonical regen (8 `focus_*` + 2 bootstrap-canonical annotation
  targets + ebnf Step B/C make-identical paths) — **ONLY `generated/regex_parser.rs` changed**

#### `.5.i.4` P1a EMISSION DESIGN (session #112, 2026-07-13 — recorded BEFORE emission; scope decisions on the STEP-0 numbers)

- **Shared decision, no census/codegen drift (the P2 `first_set.rs` precedent):** the gates (a)–(d)
  verdict AND the emission budget live in `fusibility_census.rs` as pure pub functions
  (`rule_inline_verdict`, `compute_inline_decisions`); the census reports them, codegen consumes them.
  The cycle fact and reference/occurrence counts use the census's OWN collectors — one implementation.
- **Budget (measured, not guessed):** `decided(R) = eligible(R) ∧ weight(R) ≤ 12 ∧
  weight(R)×sites(R) ≤ 192`, where `weight` = capped-transitive expansion computed bottom-up over the
  (provably acyclic) eligible subgraph and `sites` = grammar-wide reference occurrences. Basis: a
  gen-AST weight model over fresh STEP-0-pin-reproducing outcome dumps (2389/617/568 byte-exact):
  uncapped inlining costs ×4.2–14 emitted-body growth (regex) and intolerable SV growth (base 68.9MB);
  at T=12/C=192 the model says regex ×1.75 (+~1.7k body nodes) with **1179/2389 = 49% bench-entry
  exposure**, SV ×1.73, vhdl ×2.11, ebnf ×1.89. Over-budget eligible rules are LOGGED at codegen (no
  silent caps). **Re-priced falsifiable P1a expectation at this budget: ≈ −5.5–7%** (49% of the
  −9–11% frame-elision ceiling + the full needs_raw fold), decided by land-iff-faster.
- **Emission via ONE emitted engine helper** (`inlined_frame_call(rule_id, rule_name, neg_case,
  neg_strict, f)`) carrying the preserved per-frame observability VERBATIM — entry `fetch_add`,
  coverage push, furthest-position, `memoized_call` (memo PRESERVED), and the method-identical
  ✅/⚠️/❌ exit trace lines + negative-case recording — so an inlined site duplicates ONLY the child
  body and the observability protocol exists once (structurally drift-free). Emitted only when ≥1
  rule is decided.
- **Identical-by-construction body:** the method's `rule_body_inner` construction is factored into
  `generate_rule_body_inner(...)`, used by BOTH the rule method and the inlined call site; the call
  site wraps it as `|parser| { let start_pos = parser.position; #body }` (provably the method's
  binding — `memoized_call`'s miss path does not move `position` before running `f`).
- **needs_raw ride-along fold:** when the ANALYSIS annotation table (the P2 `OnceCell` copy of the
  SAME compiled resolution the parser burns in) proves `needs_raw_post/final == false` for a rule,
  the body head emits `let semantic_capture_raw_for_post = false;` instead of the two FxHash probes —
  legal for EVERY directive-free rule, method and inline alike; analysis-table compile failure keeps
  the probes (sound default).
- **Elided at inlined frames (all proven trace-/error-payload-only):** recursion-guard enter/exit
  (gate (a): acyclic ⇒ `check_cycle` keys on the rule's OWN name — absent frames of OTHER names
  cannot change Infinite/LeftRecursive verdicts; `parse_stack` labels are trace-only, and
  `try_parse`'s rollback label may name the parent — the documented V2-class delta), rule-context
  push/pop (every `rule_context_path` consumer sits inside `pgen_trace_high!` — outcome-neutral),
  the `--trace-rules` scope probe (an inlined child's name no longer activates scoping — documented
  trace-only delta), and the method call frame. NAMED residual risk: the recursion-depth ceiling
  (`parse_stack.len() ≥ 4096`) now counts fewer frames per logical descent (bounded: eligible rules
  are acyclic, so between two cycle-rule frames at most one bounded acyclic chain is elided);
  ceiling-adjacent inputs are adjudicated by the corpus real-tuple check in the battery.

#### `.5.i.4` P1b PRICING AT THE LANDED BUDGET (session #113, 2026-07-13 — recorded BEFORE emission; instrument = the census decided-exposure join)

**The instrument (landed this step; read-only analysis, no emitted-code change):** the census
outcome join (`join_outcome_counts`, `fusibility_census.rs`) filtered only on `eligible` — the
budget-DECIDED subset (the emission plan `compute_inline_decisions` actually collapses) was
model-estimated in the P1a design record (1179/2389 = 49%), never measured. `InlineExposure`
gained `decided_entries/decided_committed/decided_discarded/decided_memo_hits` (summed where the
per-rule census `decided` flag holds — the SAME shared decision map codegen consumes, drift-free
by construction) + the `INLINE-EXPOSURE-DECIDED` report line. The exposure unit test now carries
an eligible-but-OVER-BUDGET rule (14-branch alternation, weight > expansion cap 12) so the
decided sums are discriminated from the eligible sums; census module tests 13/13.

**THE MEASURED P1b SURFACE (8-pattern bench, HEAD P1a parsers, fresh dumps reproducing the
`2389/617/568` P1a pins byte-exactly):**
```
INLINE-EXPOSURE-DECIDED: grammar=regex decided_entries=1179 (49.4% of total) committed=184
                         discarded=995 memo_hits_on_decided=324
```
- The P1a design model's "1179/2389 = 49% bench-entry exposure at T=12/C=192" is now REPRODUCED
  BY DIRECT MEASUREMENT (the model and the instrument agree on the exact entry count).
- Decided body executions = 1179 − 324 = 855. Lost-hit population (P1b re-executes these) is
  leaf-dominated: `class_zero_width` 108, `class_atom` 56, `nonzero_digit` 39, `unicode_char` 29
  (top-4 = 232/324 = 72%) — all cheap post-P2 byte-switch leaves, where re-execution plausibly
  UNDERCUTS the hit-replay `node.clone()`.
- **HONEST CEILING (falsifiable, recorded before any emission; cost basis = the `.5.i.1` measured
  buckets, post-P1a bench sum ≈368–380µs):** elided per decided entry — memo dispatch probes
  (fail-set contains + tainted-map get + success-map get ≈10–14ns) × 1179 ≈ **12–17µs**; elided
  per decided SUCCESS — the memo insert (`node.clone()` + `extract_delta_since` + map insert
  ≈68–103ns, the `.5.i.1` 8–12% bucket) × successes-on-decided (bounded [committed 184, body
  executions 855]; ≈53% success share measured on `literal_simple`'s occupancy split ⇒ model
  ≈340–470) ≈ **23–48µs**; elided per decided failure — fail-set/tainted-map insert ≈5–10ns ×
  ≈385–515 ≈ **2–5µs**; MINUS the lost-hit re-execution penalty — 324 hits × net (re-execution −
  replay-clone) ≈0–40ns ≈ **0–13µs**. **Net ≈ 24–70µs ⇒ P1b expectation ≈ −6–18% geomean**,
  land-iff-faster decides; a sub-noise measurement = revert + record (the `.5.d.2` precedent).
- Named caveats: (i) the success-share scaling is a model (the dump does not decompose body
  executions into success/fail — the range is bounded by committed/body-execs both measured);
  (ii) a lost hit on a `shaped` decided rule re-executes its child calls (bounded: decided bodies
  are capped-transitive weight ≤12 and non-decided children keep their own memoized methods);
  (iii) failed probes at the same (rule, position) re-execute per probe under P1b (no fail-set) —
  bounded by the caller's branch count at that position, priced inside the lost-hit penalty band.

#### `.5.i.4` P1b EMISSION DESIGN + FIRST-CONTACT IDENTITY (session #113, 2026-07-13 — design recorded with first-contact measurements, BEFORE the speed verdict)

**The change (surgical by construction):** the emitted `inlined_frame_call` helper's dispatch —
`self.memoized_call(rule_id, f).map(|(node, _raw)| node)` → `f(self).map(|(node, _raw)| node)`.
The regenerated `regex_parser.rs` whole-file diff vs the P1a artifact is EXACTLY this one hunk
(sha `43f91b59…` → `009e1485…`). Entry `fetch_add`, coverage push, furthest-position, exit trace
lines, negative-case recording all stay verbatim; the rule METHOD keeps its `memoized_call`
untouched (method-path memoization intact for entry-relative parsing and ineligible callers).
**Result-neutrality argument (why by construction):** the memo is a PURE, taint-gated cache —
replay ≡ re-execution wherever a replay was legal (`MEMO-STORE-SOUNDNESS.2` is precisely the
proof that stale replays are evicted; the eligible subgraph is additionally directive-free and
acyclic by gates (a)/(b)). A former hit re-executes the budget-capped body whose non-decided
children keep their own memoized methods; furthest-position is a global max over territory the
insert-time execution already explored at the SAME position (the memo key includes position).
Failure-path errors propagate the body's live error instead of the fail-set's synthesized
`Backtrack{key.1}` — caller `try_parse` semantics identical; error PAYLOAD deltas on rejected
parses are trace/diagnostic-only (V2-class, documented).
**FIRST-CONTACT IDENTITY (debug candidate vs P1a, before any speed spend):**
- 8/8 bench AST dumps BYTE-IDENTICAL (`cmp` exact).
- Per-rule COMMITTED counts byte-identical on ALL 8 patterns (aggregate 617 unchanged) — exactly
  the H.10.2.2 prediction: under P1a a hit replayed the cached coverage delta, so committed
  already counted the children; under P1b re-execution pushes the same entries live.
- Raw entries 2389 → 2503 (+114, truthful: former hits re-execute bodies — growth concentrated
  in children of the hit-heavy leaves: `class_literal` +56, quoted-literal escapes +24…).
- Memo hits 568 → 314: the 324 decided-frame hits VANISH (no probes at inlined sites); +70
  child-level hits APPEAR (re-executed bodies probe their children's method memos, which replay
  answered silently under P1a). Two-way accounting, both directions truthful.
- regex cert seeds 0/7/42 `--count 40`: pins BYTE-IDENTICAL
  (`total=267 proof=9 witness=258 UNKNOWN=0 fully_certified=true`), spf `0/1/1` = baseline.
**SPEED (decisive — fat-LTO alternated 5×2000, geomean-of-mins; probes differ ONLY in
`generated/regex_parser.rs` — base probe `a774cba7…` embeds the P1a artifact `43f91b59…`,
candidate probe `0ee787e1…` embeds the P1b artifact `009e1485…`, both rebuilt fresh):**
- Per-round geomean ratios `0.9285 / 0.8805 / 0.9266 / 0.9054 / 0.8784` — candidate faster
  EVERY round (−7.2…−12.2%).
- FINAL geomean-of-best-mins **35.13µs → 31.53µs (−10.3%)**; ALL 8 patterns faster at best-mins
  (ratios 0.827–0.968; the hit-heaviest patterns win most: literal_simple ×0.827, email ×0.878,
  anchor_complex ×0.884).
- Inside the priced ≈−6–18% band (the pricing section above). Conservative claim: **≈−7–12%,
  ≈36µs → ≈32µs; cumulative 496µs → ≈32µs ≈ 15.5×.** Land-iff-faster satisfied unambiguously.

#### `.5.i.3` — P2 DEGENERATE-DISPATCH EMISSION RESULTS (session #110, 2026-07-13): LANDED — geomean **−5.3%** (≈42.0µs → ≈39.7µs), byte-identical; the STEP-0 prediction (≈−3–7%) confirmed

**The change (codegen only; engine untouched; parser-agnostic, capability-gated):**
- **Shared eligibility, no census/codegen drift:** the per-branch dispatch-first-bytes predicate moved into
  `first_set.rs` (`branch_dispatch_first_bytes` — resolved + non-nullable + non-empty + every FIRST
  terminal's first byte extractable, the exact `.5.c.2` prune-guard eligibility); the degeneracy census and
  codegen's gate are now thin consumers of the SAME function. Verified: the emitted dispatch sites match the
  census's 41 qualified rules ONE-FOR-ONE (switch-per-fn audit of the regenerated `regex_parser.rs`).
- **Codegen gate** (`degenerate_dispatch_byte_sets`, `ast_based_generator.rs`): `emit_first_set_guard`
  (= top-level + `layout_sensitivity().terminals`, gates a+b) + gate (c)/(d) via the shared predicate +
  pairwise-disjointness + gate (e) via `rule_has_branch_start_effects` and NEW
  `rule_has_branch_phase_predicates` (queried against a lazily-compiled ANALYSIS copy of the runtime
  annotation table — `analysis_runtime_annotations: OnceCell<Option<…>>`, compile-failure = blocks dispatch,
  the sound default).
- **Emission** (`generate_or_logic`): ONE `match parser.input.as_bytes()[parse_start]` with a
  `bytes… => sole-candidate` arm per branch; the candidate runs under `try_parse` with the SAME 🚪/✅
  entering/leaving trace lines; on success: transform at candidate_end (BRANCH-BROADCAST-FIX.3 order holds —
  nothing rolls back), `semantic_selected_branch_index`/`semantic_raw_content` set as before, the SAME 🏁
  selection line; on failure/no-candidate-byte/EOF: the verbatim general failure path (recovery hints /
  `Backtrack{parse_start}`) with the SAME ❌ line. ELIDED at qualified sites: the n-arm guard-scan loop, the
  tournament semantic checkpoint clone, `extract_delta_since` + C3-B `rollback_to_labeled` + winner
  `apply_delta`, the `should_take` cascade, `best_*` bookkeeping, the partition-offset computation, and the
  per-successful-branch predicate table probes (gate (e): provably none exist).
- **Speed (decisive):** baseline = the on-disk P0 release probe sha `23bf4f30…` (sha-verified = the recorded
  `.5.i.2` candidate, pinned ≈42.0µs) vs candidate sha `7057eb61…` (fat-LTO, new emission), alternated
  5×2000: per-round geomean-of-mins ratios `0.9500/0.9369/0.9574/0.9573/0.9503` — candidate faster EVERY
  round, zero overlap; FINAL geomean-of-best-mins **41.89µs → 39.68µs (−5.3%)**; per-pattern best-min ratios
  0.923–0.974 (ALL 8 faster; 4/5 rounds all-8-faster per-round). Inside the STEP-0 prediction band ≈−3–7%.
- **Byte-identity/battery:** 8/8 bench `--parse-dump-ast-pretty` dumps IDENTICAL old-vs-new; 8/8 raw AND
  committed rule-count dumps IDENTICAL (counters/coverage preserved); equivalence gate GREEN (interpreter ==
  generated, all grammars — the too-tight-first-set oracle); combinator gate GREEN (incl. `a|ab` — overlap ⇒
  correctly NOT degenerate); semantic gate GREEN; regex cert seeds 0/7/42 `--count 40` pins BYTE-IDENTICAL
  (`total=267 proof=9 witness=258 UNKNOWN=0 fully_certified=true`, spf `0/1/1`); ast-shape GREEN; duality-hunt
  GREEN; PCRE2 compile-oracle GREEN; lib unit tests 766/766; clippy strict-source clean. **All 9 non-regex
  parsers regen BYTE-IDENTICAL** (sha-compared old-vs-new codegen full regen; regex is the only
  terminal-ws-sensitive grammar) — capability-gated, never grammar-name-gated.
- **Observability delta (as documented at open):** at the 41 sites the C3-B cleanup rollback event and the
  winner-delta replay vanish (protocol that provably no longer exists); 🚪/✅/❌/🏁 lines preserved
  (trace-verified on `letter`: dispatch enters branch 46/52 directly — the same sole branch the guards left —
  and the 🏁 line is unchanged); rule-entry counters, committed counts, coverage, furthest-position, memo
  behavior all unchanged (proven by the identical count dumps + cert pins).
