#### `.5.i.3` STEP-0 — DEGENERACY-CENSUS SCOUT RESULTS (session #110, 2026-07-13): 41 qualified sites, 12.8% entry exposure, 36.9% of tournament loop iterations; falsifiable prediction ≈ −3–7%

**The instrument (landed this step, read-only analysis — no codegen/parse-behavior change):**
`--report-fusibility-census` extended with the per-site DEGENERACY classification
(`fusibility_census.rs`): per choice-site branch, the admissible dispatch FIRST bytes
(`first_bytes` — EXACTLY the codegen prune-guard eligibility: resolved + non-nullable + every FIRST
terminal's first byte extractable, via the shared `first_set` module); per site, the P2 gate verdict
`degenerate_dispatch` + NAMED `degeneracy_blockers` (R1 nesting / R2 layout / per-branch undecided /
per-byte overlap with branch lists / branch-phase predicates / branch-start effects — the predicate
and effect facts queried against the SAME compiled runtime-annotation table the generated parser
consults, never re-derived). New output lines `DEGENERACY-CENSUS:` (+ blocker histogram, top-level
sites) and — with the outcome-counts join — `DEGENERACY-EXPOSURE:` (raw/committed/discarded entries
on rules whose top-level site qualifies; each rule counted once). 3 new unit tests (qualified
disjoint site / named blockers incl. overlap+nullable+R2 / pure-verdict predicate+effect+nesting
naming); 9/9 module tests green.

**Cross-validation:** the joined OUTCOME-SHARE reproduces the `.5.h.1b` pins BYTE-EXACTLY on
freshly-dumped outcome counts for the 8 bench patterns
(`total_entries=2389 committed=617 discarded=1773 on_encodable=830 on_structural=943
committed_on_encodable=215 ceiling≈1.53x`, `unmatched=0`, `committed_overshoot` quiet).

**THE MEASURED P2 SURFACE (8-pattern bench, HEAD parsers):**
```
DEGENERACY-CENSUS:   grammar=regex top_level_sites=112 degenerate_dispatch=41
DEGENERACY-EXPOSURE: degenerate_site_entries=306 (12.8% of total) committed=124 discarded=182
loop iterations at degenerate sites = 5258 of 14237 top-level tournament iterations (36.9%)
```
- The 41 qualified sites are the single-char alternation LEAVES: `letter` (52 branches, 57 entries,
  2964 iterations — the dominator), `nonzero_digit` (9br/64e/576it), `simple_escape_letter_strict`
  (34br/476it), `class_safe_special` (30br/420it), `digit` (10br/400it), `quant_base` (4br/63e/252it), …
- Blocker histogram (top-level sites): first-byte OVERLAP between branches ×116 occurrences (the
  `\`-led escape cluster and `(`-led group cluster — multi-char discrimination territory, out of P2
  scope), branch NOT first-byte-decided ×41 (nullable / unresolved regex-token / cycle).
- All-grammar static table: **regex 41/112 is the ONLY grammar with a degenerate surface** — every
  other grammar has `degenerate_dispatch=0` (R2: only regex declares terminal whitespace-sensitivity;
  svpp is regex_tokens-only). Same effective-scope shape as the landed `.5.c.2` — the mechanism stays
  parser-agnostic + capability-gated; other grammars regen byte-identical by construction.
- **HONEST CEILING (falsifiable, recorded before emission):** per qualified Or-body execution the
  emission elides the branch guard-scan loop (5258 iterations ≈ 2–4ns each ≈ 10–21µs), the winner's
  tournament protocol (124 successes × checkpoint-clone/extract_delta/rollback/apply/should_take
  ≈ 30–60ns ≈ 4–7µs), and the compile-time-elided branch-predicate table probes. Against the ≈420µs
  post-P0 bench total ⇒ predicted geomean **≈ −3–7%** (≈42.0µs → ≈39–41µs). First-byte dispatch kills
  NO rule entries beyond the `.5.c.2` guards (confirmed: the 830 encodable discards live at
  OVERLAP-blocked sites) — the win is pure protocol elision, as the leaf spec recorded at open.
  ⛔ land-iff-faster decides; a sub-noise measurement = REVERT + record (the `.5.d.2` precedent).

#### `.5.i.2` — P0 LAZY/NO-ALLOC PROTOCOL HYGIENE RESULTS (session #109, 2026-07-13): LANDED — geomean **−25.8%** (≈56.7µs → ≈42.0µs), byte-identical, observability-preserving

**The change (parser-agnostic; engine + codegen + interpreter; all parsers inherit):**
- **(a) LAZY rollback naming (the V2 surface, measured −14.7%):** new `RollbackLabel<'a>` enum
  (`semantic_runtime.rs`, `Copy` — pointers + integers only) + `rollback_to_labeled(checkpoint, label)`;
  `rollback_to_named` is now a thin wrapper (`Str`/`Unspecified`) kept for compatibility. The generated
  `try_parse` Err arm captures the owning rule as a FREE `Option<&'static str>` copy from `parse_stack`
  (which has been `Vec<(&'static str, usize)>` since RGX-0073) and passes `RollbackLabel::TryParseErr`;
  the C3-B per-successful-branch cleanup passes `RollbackLabel::C3bBranchCleanup{rule,branch,total}`.
  `materialize()` runs ONLY inside the trace-enabled branch of `rollback_to_labeled` (the whole label+chain
  block is now explicitly gated on `trace_enabled(High)`), and reproduces the previous eager strings
  byte-for-byte. Kills 2×`String` per failed speculation + 1×`format!` per successful tournament branch
  (2676 rollbacks/bench).
- **(b) No-Vec branch order + conditional partition-group (the V3 surface, measured −6.5%):** the
  multi-branch tournament emission (`generate_or_logic`) now iterates `(evaluation_step + offset) %
  branch_count` directly — provably the `rotate_left(offset)` sequence, identity order when offset=0 —
  instead of collecting an `evaluation_order: Vec<usize>` per Or-body execution; and
  `effective_deterministic_partition_group` (an unconditional String per Or-body — `.to_string()`/`format!`)
  is now computed ONLY inside the effectively-enabled branch. Behavior identical under ALL runtime modes
  (AnnotationDriven/ForceEnabled/ForceDisabled — the runtime check is preserved, unlike the per-rule-entry
  partition-event elision which is annotation-gated at codegen).
- **(c) No-alloc rule-context stack (the V1 surface, measured −4.3%):** `current_rule_context_stack` is now
  `Vec<Cow<'static, str>>`; new `push_rule_context_static(&'static str)` pushes `Cow::Borrowed` — used by
  the emitted rule IIFE/transaction helper (whose `rule_name` param is now `&'static str`; every call site
  passes the rule-name literal) AND by the interpreter (its names are already interned `&'static str`), so
  BOTH parse paths push allocation-free. The allocating `push_rule_context(&str)` is KEPT
  signature-compatible — deliberately, so the canonical `make focus_*` regen toolchain (which embeds the
  PREVIOUS generation's parsers) still compiles: a breaking signature would have forced the non-canonical
  bootstrap regen that caused the `.5.i.1.t1` null→"null" drift incident. V4/V5 stay OUT per the
  observability doctrine (P3 decides per-region later).

**Baseline identity note (MEMORY drift corrected):** the on-disk release probe at session start was a STALE
pre-regen build (sha `a6bf0553…` — it predated the `.5.i.1` post-incident canonical regen); rebuilding from
clean HEAD reproduced the recorded canonical baseline sha `d43bc6a0…` BIT-FOR-BIT (deterministic build
confirmed) and its geomean ≈55.8µs (2000/200) reproduces the pinned ≈56.7µs. MEMORY.md's ON-DISK note now
names the candidate build.

**Emission spot-check (regenerated `regex_parser.rs`):** `push_rule_context_static` ×214 / allocating
remnants 0 / `TryParseErr` ×1 / `C3bBranchCleanup` ×993 / eager `format!` remnants 0 /
`rotate_left`+`evaluation_order` 0 / modulo-order present.

#### `.5.i.1` — STEP-0 COST-DECOMPOSITION CENSUS RESULTS (session #108, 2026-07-13): the ~236ns/entry decomposed; P0 discovered; P0–P4 ceilings + build order fixed

**Instruments (landed this slice, parser-agnostic):** the `.5.h.1b` outcome dump now also reports the
parse's SEMANTIC-STORE COUNTER DELTAS (`store_counters` in the JSON: rollbacks / facts_emitted /
facts_rolled_back / scopes± / predicate_evaluations) via the emitted `semantic_runtime_state()` accessor
every generated parser already has — lib-only (`parser_registry.rs`, both the macro path and the regex
worker-thread path), baseline-delta discipline, zero cost unset, NO regen needed. `rollbacks` = failed
`try_parse` speculations + C3-B per-successful-branch cleanups — the speculation-count side per-rule
entry counts cannot see.

**Counts (8-pattern bench, canonical parsers — every prior cross-check reproduces):** 2389 raw entries /
617 committed / **1776 body executions** (661 success + 1115 failure memo inserts) / **613 memo hits** /
**2676 rollbacks** (more than one per raw entry!) / 48 facts emitted (anchor_complex + capture_groups
only). Bench total ≈ 564µs ⇒ ≈236ns/entry average (reproduces `.5.e`/`.5.h.1`).

**THE MEASURED ABLATION TABLE** (each variant an isolated measurement-only build, REVERTED after —
nothing landed; each measured vs the canonical fat-LTO baseline (sha `d43bc6a0…`, geomean ≈56.7µs —
reproduces the pinned ≈56.8µs) ALTERNATED 5 rounds × 2000 samples; each verified **AST-byte-identical**
on all 8 patterns before its number was trusted):

| # | bucket stripped | mechanism (WHY it exists per entry) | Δ geomean | ≈ns/event |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V1 | per-entry rule-context String | `push_rule_context` does `rule_name.to_string()` on EVERY entry — trace-naming payload | **−4.3%**¹ | ~10/entry |
| V2 | rollback-naming strings | failed `try_parse`: `entry.0.to_string()` + `format!("… (try_parse Err)")`; EVERY successful tournament branch: `format!("… (C3-B branch i/n cleanup)")` — consumed ONLY under trace | **−14.7%** | ~31/rollback × 2676 |
| V3 | tournament bookkeeping allocs | `evaluation_order: Vec = (0..n).collect()` + partition-group String (`format!("rule.{}")`) built UNCONDITIONALLY per Or-body execution (partitioning disabled or not) | **−6.5%** | ~40/Or-body |
| V4 | observability bundle | rule-call `fetch_add` + coverage branch + furthest-position + `trace_rules` probe + every `trace_enabled()` gate | **−3.4%** | ~8/entry |
| V5 | recursion guard | `enter`/`exit` stack push/pop every entry + `check_cycle` linear stack scan on recursive rules (isolation: Err-arm string work held constant) | **−3.8%** | ~9/entry |
| **V6** | **ALL five combined** | | **−31.7%** (≈56.7µs → ≈38.7µs) | ~75/entry |

¹ V1 measured on the pre-canonical pair (both sides same parser source — pair-clean; anchor differs ~1%).
V6 (−31.7%) EXCEEDS the product of the five (−29.1%) — allocator-pressure relief compounds (~+2.6pp
synergy). Per-round ratios all-rounds-faster for every variant (V2: 0.854/0.856/0.840/0.850/0.864; V6:
0.690/0.673/0.683/0.689/0.680; rest in the session log).

**Model + profiler for the non-ablatable buckets** (unit microbench: FxHash probe 3.2–4.7ns, memo
insert+remove ~9ns, small-String alloc ~10ns, `format!` ~38–47ns, Vec-collect(7) ~17ns; profiler leaf
self-time: alloc-family ≈42% + memops ≈10%, `directives_for_rule` 1.5%, serde/BTree ≈5%, clones 3.9%,
memcmp 3.5%, expression-interp ≥2.2%, guard 0.4%):
- **memo machinery ≈17–22%**: ~4 probes/entry (fail-set, tainted-map, memo.get ×2) ≈6.8% + success-insert
  `node.clone()` + delta extraction + insert ≈8–12% + hit-replay clones ≈2–3%.
- **annotation-table probes ≈4–6%**: `needs_raw_post/final` ×2/body + `directives_for_rule` +
  `branch_predicates_for_rule(_branch)` ×2/successful-branch + `has_rule`.
- **value-constraint expression RE-PARSING ≈2–4%**: `evaluate_relational_expression` string-splits
  (`split_semantic_top_level` on `||`/`&&`, paren-trim, operator scan, `Vec<&str>` allocs) on EVERY
  parse-time evaluation — the `.5.g` cache never reached expression PAYLOADS (compile-time foldable, P4).
- **serde JSON output ≈5–6%**; **node build/arena residual ≈4–6%**; **structural remainder ≈15–20%**
  (byte matching, dispatch control flow, call frames — the only part P1/P2 restructuring must touch).

**THE ≈236ns/entry, DECOMPOSED (bench-average):** ≈75ns unconditional waste (V6-measured: naming strings
~35, tournament allocs ~15, context String ~10, observability ~8, guard ~9) + ≈40–50ns memo machinery +
≈10–14ns annotation-table probes + ≈12–14ns serde (amortized) + ≈5–8ns expression interpretation +
≈10–14ns node build + ≈40–55ns structural parse work.

**⇒ P0–P4 CEILINGS + THE BUILD ORDER FIXED BY MEASUREMENT:**

| pass | surface (measured/derived) | falsifiable ceiling | analysis needed |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P0 (NEW) — lazy/no-alloc protocol hygiene** | V1+V2+V3: trace-naming payloads built eagerly for a trace that is off; Vec built to iterate 0..n | **≈−25%** (compose ≈0.763 + synergy; observability-PRESERVING: materialize names lazily only when trace consumes them) | **NONE** — plain engineering, all parsers inherit |
| P2 — predictive dispatch | 830 encodable discarded entries (`.5.h.1b`) + their rollback share (V2-adjacent) | ≈1.53× pre-P0; shrinks toward ~1.3–1.4× post-P0 (per-discard cost drops) | FIRST-set disjointness (exists: `.5.c.2` + census) |
| P1 — cascade/wrapper inlining | ~290 of 617 committed entries are pass-through wrapper frames (letter 47, literal 34, literal_char 34, escape chain ~90, entry_* 24…) | ≈−12% (≈290 × ~236ns) pre-P0; relatively larger post-P0 | single-caller/single-branch closure (cheap) |
| P3 — selective machinery | memo ≈17–22% + guard 3.8% (V5) + counters/coverage 3.4% (V4, ⛔ doctrine-gated: always-on dashboards) + checkpoint/transaction elision | ≈−15–25% on the memo-selectivity side | effect-freedom + recursion reach (exists in fusibility census) |
| P4 — value folding | serde 5–6% + node clones 4–6% + expression pre-compilation 2–4% | ≈−12–16% | return-annotation shape analysis (exists) |

**Recommended order: P0 → P2 → P1 → P3 → P4** — P0 first (no analysis, biggest cheap win, shrinks
every later pass's baseline honestly), then the two entry-count killers in measured-surface order (P2's
830 discards + P1's ~290 wrapper frames), then selectivity and folding. V4/V5 components stay OUT of P0:
always-on counters + furthest-position power the standing observability doctrine — their elision is
P3's per-region decision, not a blanket strip. Composed honest destination if every ceiling lands near
its measurement: ≈56.7µs → ≈15–20µs on this bench (before `.7` batch/parse-cache) — still short of
PCRE2's ≈0.57µs by an order of magnitude; the entry-count product (P1+P2) and `.7` remain the road's
destination levers. ⛔ HARD CONSTRAINT unchanged per increment: land iff measured faster + byte-identical
under the full oracle battery.

     shape_only = scan is a prefix discriminator only — the winning descent stays), the branch closure rules +

  re-adjudicates on the number.

#### `.5.h.1b` — STEP-0b CHOICE-SITE CENSUS RESULTS (session #107, 2026-07-13): tools + measurements + the verdict

**The instruments built (read-only; no parse-behavior change):**
1. Codegen accessor `exercised_rule_entry_counts()` — the committed-count histogram fold of the transactional
   coverage stack (sibling of `exercised_rule_names`; zero hot-path cost — no new parse-time work; pinned by the
   `transactional_parse_coverage_wiring_is_emitted_at_codegen` needle). All 11 parsers REGENERATED (debug regen via
   the frontend/bootstrap binaries — the accessor-before-artifact chicken-and-egg resolved by building the
   `ebnf_dual_run`-only pipeline first, whose feature-gated build excludes `parser_registry`).
2. `parseability_probe --parse <g> <input> --dump-rule-outcome-counts-json F` — raw + COMMITTED per-rule counts
   (macro-shared expansion with the `.5.h.1` dump; regex worker-thread capture mirrored; opt-in thread-local,
   unset ⇒ coverage stays disabled ⇒ byte-identical behavior).
3. `ast_pipeline <g>.ebnf --report-fusibility-census` extended: the CHOICE-SITE census (every Or site ≥2 branches:
   encodable-branch subset via the `.5.h.1` shape classifier, per-branch text tier, sole-reference attribution) +
   `--fusibility-outcome-counts F1,…` join → `OUTCOME-SHARE` (committed/discarded × encodable/structural
   decomposition + per-site sole-attributable discards). 2 new unit tests (mixed-site classification + join
   decomposition; 6/6 module tests green).

**Cross-validations (the instrument agrees with every independent prior measurement):**
- `test` raw dump: `total_entries=50`, `atom=10` — byte-identical to the `.5.h.1` record (regen'd parsers included).
- `test` committed = **24** — EXACTLY the `.5.e` memo-stats "45 = 24 success + 21 cached-failure" success half,
  per-rule (`atom`/`letter`/`literal`/`literal_char`/`piece` ×4 + the 4 top-level rules + `regex`).
- Determinism: outcome dump re-run byte-identical (`cmp` clean). Verdict-neutrality: 8/8 bench patterns same rc
  with and without the dump.
- The absolute per-rule-fusion bound REPRODUCES across instruments: kill-everything-encodable = 830 discarded +
  215 committed = 1045 entries ⇒ 2389/1344 ≈ **1.78×** — the SAME 1045/≈1.8× the `.5.h.1` entry-share computed
  from static tiers (83+495+467). Two independent decompositions, one number.

**THE MEASURED VERDICT (8-pattern bench, uniform per-entry cost model):**
```
OUTCOME-SHARE: grammar=regex files=8 total_entries=2389 committed=617 discarded=1773
               (on_encodable=830 on_structural=943) committed_on_encodable=215 ceiling≈1.53x
CHOICE-SITE-CENSUS: grammar=regex sites=148 (top_level=112) with_encodable_subset=133 all_encodable=107
```
- **74.2% of ALL bench entries (1773/2389) are DISCARDED failing-probe work** — the probing waste is real and huge.
- But only **830 of the 1773 sit on shape-ENCODABLE rules** — the part a merged-choice/site DFA can answer.
  **Measured increment-(ii) ceiling ≈ 1.53×** — and that is OPTIMISTIC in wall-clock terms (memo-hit re-entries,
  which are cheap, are counted at full weight; scan calls are costed at ~0).
- The other **943 discarded entries sit on STRUCTURAL rules** (`atom` 111, `class_atom` 66, `quantifier` 50,
  `quant_base` 45, `piece` 27, …) whose language is NOT regular (they reference the recursive `group`/class
  cycle) — undecidable by an exact site DFA; only prefix-APPROXIMATION discrimination (a new, unscoped increment
  class) could touch them. Top discarded-encodable rules: `unicode_char` 158, `class_zero_width` 134,
  `zero_width` 87, `nonzero_digit` 56, `literal` 51.
- Top sites by sole-attributable discards (lower bounds): `atom@or#0` 94 (24 branches, 7 encodable),
  `literal@or#0` 46 (ALL-encodable), `zero_width@or#0` 36 (ALL), `piece@or#0` 23. `committed_overshoot=1`
  (a known lookahead-success + memo-replay artifact; loud, saturating-sub handled).
- Static all-grammar site census (encodable-subset sites / total): regex 133/148, semantic_annotation 28/38,
  ebnf 29/38, svpp 7/9, return_annotation 5/11, json 3/5, SV 9/535, rtl_frontend 1/40, vhdl 0/39,
  rtl_const_expr 0/9, scratch 1/1 — the non-regex grammars have essentially no merged-choice surface either.

#### `.5.h.1.t1` — the `spf 0/1/1` residual ADJUDICATED (session #107, 2026-07-13): label gap FIXED; over-generation ORACLE-CLASSIFIED

**(a) The FAILURE-LABEL gap — FIXED (one-line registry wiring).**
- WHY+WHERE: `parse_error()` (`parser_registry.rs:912`) dispatches through the registry entry's `parse_detail`
  hook; the regex entry had `parse_detail: None` (never wired when G.4.7 added the hook per-grammar — only
  SV/svpp/scratch were wired), so `main.rs`'s cert failure-labeler fell back to
  `"(no detail-capable parser registered)"`. The existing `parse_with_regex_detail` already has the exact
  `ParseDetailFn` signature.
- FIX: `parse_detail: Some(parse_with_regex_detail)` (+ the semantic note: the detail fn's PCRE2 compile
  contract never runs on this pathway — it is only consulted for samples whose grammar parse failed, where the
  deterministic re-parse fails before the contract).
- Before → after (seed 7): `[0] error: (no detail-capable parser registered)` →
  `[0] error: Parser did not consume full input at position 90`. Cert headline byte-identical
  (`total=267 proof=9 witness=258 UNKNOWN=0 fully_certified=true`, spf unchanged).

**(b) The 117-byte over-generation — ORACLE-CLASSIFIED as the \Q-quoting ROUND-TRIP-ABSORPTION class (gen-side; the parser is RIGHT).**
- The sample's tail is `…(*PRUNE)|(?(51)\Q\Q\Q)*?^\Q3 T 8é\E`. Position 90 = the `(` of the conditional. The
  `\Q` opened after `(?(51)` quotes to the FIRST following `\E` — which is the FINAL `\E` of the pattern — so the
  conditional's closing `)` (and `*?^…`) are absorbed as quoted literal content and the group never closes.
- **PCRE2 oracle verdict (pcre2test 10.47, the exact sample):** `Failed: error 114 at offset 117: missing
  closing parenthesis` — PCRE2 quotes the same way and rejects. The parser's reject is PCRE2-FAITHFUL
  (accept/reject parity holds); the defect is that the GENERATOR emitted the pattern: a quoted-literal piece
  generated inside a conditional whose rendered text, on re-parse, absorbs the enclosing group's structural
  close. Same class as the svpp `H.5.5` round-trip hazard; the gen-side-has-no-`lacks_fact`-prune family
  ([[project_gen_side_no_lacks_fact_branch_prune]] — structural grammar peel is the preferred fix shape).
- **Adjudication: DOCUMENTED-TRACKED, fix DEFERRED.** The structural fix (position-restricting the
  quoted-literal/unterminated-quoting forms so generated content cannot absorb following structural text) is
  regex.ebnf surgery with a full-battery + book/contract lockstep cost, while the residual is bounded and stable
  (spf ≤1 per seed, unchanged across sessions #91–#107) and the §G.1 road pick pending with the director owns
  regex priorities. The ⛔ battery continues to hold spf-NEUTRALITY vs the `0/1/1` baseline; this class re-opens
  as its own leaf if the residual ever grows or the director prioritizes duality-tightening.
